TL;DR: 本文提出MAGE-Vein框架，通过多实例多任务学习融合三根手指特征并同时进行性别分类，在402名受试者的均衡数据集上实现了6.12年的平均绝对误差和0.880的相关性，证明了指静脉图像可用于年龄估计，此前失败源于数据集偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指静脉图像年龄估计一直被广泛认为不可行，主要原因在于公开数据集存在严重的群体偏差以及性别等生理混杂因素。作者旨在推翻这一传统共识，证明指静脉模态同样可用于年龄估计。

Method: 提出MAGE-Vein多实例多任务学习框架：1) 混合特征级融合三个手指的特征，抑制局部成像噪声，提取鲁棒的衰老结构特征；2) 同步优化性别分类任务，引导网络消除性别特异性血管变异。

Result: 在402名受试者的群体均衡数据集上，MAGE-Vein达到平均绝对误差6.12年，相关系数0.880。

Conclusion: 结果推翻了指静脉模态在年龄估计上的传统共识，证明此前估计失败的主要原因是公开数据集存在偏差（伪影），而非指静脉模态本身的局限性。

Abstract: Age estimation from finger vein images has been widely considered impractical due to severe demographic biases in public datasets and physiological confounding factors like gender. To overcome these limitations, we propose MAGE-Vein, a novel multi-instance, multi-task learning framework. Our approach extracts robust structural aging signs by employing a hybrid feature-level fusion of three fingers, effectively suppressing local imaging noise. Furthermore, simultaneous optimization of gender classification conditions the network to effectively eliminate gender-specific vascular variations. Evaluated on a demographically balanced dataset of 402 subjects, MAGE-Vein achieves a mean absolute error of 6.12 years and a correlation of 0.880. Our results not only overturn the conventional consensus regarding the limitations of the finger vein modality but also demonstrate that previous estimation failures were primarily artifacts of biased public datasets. Our code is available at https://github.com/gsisaoki/MAGE-Vein.

</details>


### [21] [DINO-VPT: Hierarchical Visual Prompt Tuning for Joint Physical-Digital Face Anti-Spoofing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20900)
*Pierre Gallin-Martel,Mika Feng,Koichi Ito,Takafumi Aoki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DINO-VPT，一种轻量级纯视觉框架，通过层次化视觉提示调优和提示路由网络（PRN）实现统一人脸反欺骗（FAS），无需多模态融合，在UniAttackData基准上达到优于现有VLM方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉-语言模型（VLM）的统一人脸反欺骗方法依赖复杂的多模态融合和外部文本编码器，模型复杂度高，亟需更轻量、高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出DINO-VPT框架，采用纯视觉架构，通过提示路由网络（PRN）根据输入特征动态注入层次化视觉提示，实现对多种欺骗伪影的解耦，无需多模态融合。

Result: 在UniAttackData基准上，DINO-VPT相比现有最先进的VLM方法取得了更高的准确率。

Conclusion: 合理设计的纯视觉架构在统一人脸反欺骗任务中可以达到甚至超越多模态方法的性能，表明多模态监督并非实现统一FAS的必要条件。

Abstract: With the increasing diversity of spoofing attacks, there is a growing demand for unified Face Anti-Spoofing (FAS) models capable of detecting both physical and digital threats. While existing Vision-Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate high generalization in this context, they heavily rely on complex multimodal fusion and external text encoders. In this paper, we propose DINO-VPT, a lightweight, vision-only framework leveraging hierarchical visual prompt tuning. By dynamically injecting prompts conditioned on input features via a Prompt Routing Network (PRN), our method effectively disentangles diverse spoofing artifacts without requiring multimodal fusion. Evaluations on the UniAttackData benchmark demonstrate that DINO-VPT achieves higher accuracy than state-of-the-art VLM-based methods. Our results indicate that a properly structured vision-only architecture can achieve state-of-the-art performance in unified FAS without the need for multimodal supervision.

</details>


### [22] [Sidewalk Moments: Are Richer Representations Always More Human-Aligned? Evidence from City-Walk Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20903)
*Liu Liu,Freya Huying Tan,Fábio Duarte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过分析61个第一人称城市步行视频（超过5万个10秒片段），比较了四种表征模态（时空视频特征、时间平均图像TAI、音频嵌入、文本语义描述）与人类对城市参与度判断的对齐程度。研究发现，在连续对齐中视频特征最优，但在二元分类任务（高/低参与度）中，TAI表现与视频持平甚至更优。人类实验证实了这一结果，表明更丰富的视觉表征并不必然更符合人类判断，感知驱动的时间压缩可作为全视频编码的有效替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究更丰富的视觉表征是否必然产生更符合人类判断的城市参与度度量指标，挑战"表征越丰富越符合人类感知"的默认假设。

Method: 使用61个YouTube第一人称城市步行视频，分割为超过5万个10秒片段，提取四种模态特征（时空视频特征、TAI、音频嵌入、文本语义描述），通过Spearman相关分析和二元分类实验（多个分类器和分位数阈值）评估与人类判断的对齐程度，并通过Amazon Mechanical Turk进行独立的两选一强制选择实验验证。

Result: 连续对齐中视频特征最优，但二元分类任务中TAI表现匹配或优于视频；人类实验证实TAI与完整视频的参与度判断准确率相当，文本表现显著较差，音频接近随机水平。差距分析显示视频在活动驱动场景中占优，TAI在构图驱动场景中更符合人类判断。

Conclusion: 更丰富的表征不一定更符合人类感知；在二元分类范式下，感知驱动的时间压缩（如TAI）可作为全视频编码的有效替代方案。

Abstract: We examine whether richer visual representations yield more human-aligned measures of urban engagement, using 61 first-person city-walk videos from YouTube segmented into over 50,000 ten-second clips and represented across four modalities: spatiotemporal video features, temporally averaged images (TAIs), audio embeddings, and text-based semantic descriptions. Spearman correlation analysis reveals the expected ordering along the temporal-richness continuum, with video features showing the strongest continuous alignment. However, this ordering breaks down under binary classification of high- versus low-engagement moments (the paradigm most commonly used to train perceptual scoring models), where TAIs consistently match or outperform video across most classifiers and quantile thresholds. An independent two-alternative forced-choice study on Amazon Mechanical Turk confirms that this parity reflects human judgment: participants identified engaging moments with comparable accuracy from TAIs and full video clips, while text performed substantially worse and audio remained near chance. Gap analysis reveals a functional dissociation: video features are advantaged in activity-driven scenes with dynamic content, whereas TAIs better align with human judgments in composition-driven scenes dominated by stable spatial structure. These findings challenge the assumption that richer representations are inherently more human-aligned, and suggest that perceptually grounded temporal compression can be a principled alternative to full video encoding.

</details>


### [23] [FA-LAM: Focus-Aware Large Avatar Model for One-Shot 4D Animatable Gaussian Head](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20922)
*Yingdong Hu,Yisheng He,Yiming Jiang,Zehong Lin,Steven Hoi,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FA-LAM提出了一种关注感知的大型头像模型，用于一次性的可动画化高斯头部创建，同时支持静态3D和动态4D全头重建。通过分析注意力机制和重构-动画训练流程中的问题，引入对称语义注意力正则化和双阶段训练管道，并支持多视角和流式4D重建，显著提升了面部细节和大视角下的重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进方法在3D全头生成中面临两个主要问题：(1) 注意力激活不正确且带有噪声；(2) 重构任务与动画任务之间存在目标冲突，导致重建质量受损。

Method: (1) 对称语义注意力正则化策略，利用人头的语义信息和结构对称性修正注意力激活；(2) 双阶段训练管道，将大视角幻觉能力与动画能力分离到不同模块中，解耦重构与动画目标；(3) 核心自回归修改与可见性感知令牌融合，实现高效、内存友好的多视角和流式4D重建。

Result: FA-LAM能够以更优质量重建可动画化的高斯全头，尤其在精细面部区域和大视角场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过解决注意力噪声和任务冲突两大核心问题，FA-LAM在一次性可动画化全头重建任务上实现了领先性能，并拓展了多视角及流式4D重建能力。

Abstract: We propose FA-LAM, a Focus-Aware Large Avatar Model for one-shot animatable Gaussian head creation, while simultaneously enabling static 3D and dynamic 4D full-head recovery. The core of our method lies in a thorough analysis of the attention mechanisms and the entangled reconstruction and animation training pipeline adopted by prior state-of-the-art approaches. Our analysis identifies two main factors that compromise the quality of 3D full-head generation: (1) incorrect and noisy attention activations, and (2) conflicts between the tasks of reconstruction and animation. To address the first issue, we introduce a symmetric and semantic attention regularization strategy that leverages the inherent semantics and structural symmetry of human heads. To disentangle the objectives of reconstruction and animation, we develop a novel dual-phase training pipeline that separates the model's capabilities for large-view hallucination and animation into distinct modules. Moreover, we enhance our model to support multi-view and streaming 4D reconstruction in an efficient and memory-friendly manner through a core autoregressive modification with tailored visibility-aware token fusion. Collectively, these innovations enable FA-LAM to reconstruct animatable Gaussian full heads with superior quality, particularly in fine facial regions and large viewing angles.

</details>


### [24] [MagicMakeup: A Region-Controllable Diffusion Transformer for High-Fidelity Makeup-Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20924)
*Ziyi Wang,Siming Zheng,Yang Yang,Shusong Xu,Hao Zhang,Bo Li,Changqing Zou,Peng-Tao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MagicMakeup提出了一种基于扩散Transformer的区域可控高保真妆容迁移框架，通过Token对齐区域门控（解决像素-注意力错位）和跨模态感知引导（解耦迁移/保留概念），并构建了1024×1024的高分辨率数据集，在区域可控性、妆容 fidelity 和身份保留方面显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的全脸编辑方法在强区域可控性、妆容 fidelity 和身份保留三方面面临挑战，原因包括：(i) 像素-注意力错位导致向非目标区域溢出；(ii) 双图像条件下迁移/保留概念不清晰，导致妆容属性与身份耦合；(iii) 缺乏身份一致且区域标注的高分辨率数据集用于细粒度监督。

Method: MagicMakeup 框架包含三个核心组件：(1) Token对齐区域门控（Token-Aligned Region Gating），将像素掩码与注意力对齐并施加区域特定的logit门控；(2) 跨模态感知引导（Cross-Modal Perception Guidance），对齐文本与图像特征以增强跨模态概念感知；(3) 设计了一套1024×1024数据对的生成流程，通过区域特定妆容去除构建统一基准数据集。

Result: 大量定量和定性实验表明，MagicMakeup在区域可控性、妆容 fidelity 和身份保留方面均有提升，且在风格、种族和姿态上表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MagicMakeup通过空间约束和概念解耦，有效解决了现有扩散方法在妆容迁移中的关键瓶颈，实现了高保真、区域可控的妆容迁移，并提供了统一的高分辨率基准。

Abstract: Makeup-transfer applies the reference makeup to the source face while preserving the source identity. Despite advances in full-face editing by diffusion-based methods, strong regional controllability, makeup fidelity, and identity preservation remain challenging. The reasons are (i) pixel-to-attention misalignment that causes spillover into non-target areas and weakens regional control; (ii) unclear transfer/preservation concept separation under two-image conditioning, leading to coupling between makeup attributes and identity; and (iii) the lack of a high-resolution dataset that is identity-consistent and region-labeled for fine-grained supervision. In this paper, we propose MagicMakeup, a diffusion transformer-based framework for region-controllable and high-fidelity makeup transfer, built on spatial constraints and concept disentanglement. To enable precise region-specific editing while preserving identity, we propose Token-Aligned Region Gating, which aligns pixel masks with attention and applies region-specific logit gating. To clarify the concepts of transfer and preservation, we further introduce Cross-Modal Perception Guidance, which aligns text and image features to enhance cross-modal concept perception. We also design a pipeline for the generation of 1024 x 1024 data pairs through region-specific makeup removal and establish a unified benchmark in synthetic and real settings. Extensive quantitative and qualitative experiments show that MagicMakeup improves regional controllability, makeup fidelity, and identity preservation, with strong robustness across styles, races, and poses.

</details>


### [25] [Ms. Forcing: Efficient Streaming Video Generation with Multi-Scale Patchification and Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20940)
*Zekun Li,Xiaoyan Cong,Hongyu Li,Zhiyang Dou,Chuan Guo,Abhay Mittal,Sizhe An,Srinath Sridhar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Ms.Forcing通过多尺度分块和注意力机制，根据噪声水平调整空间粒度，在流式视频生成中实现39.6%的加速（22.84 FPS），同时提升视频质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频扩散模型（如滚动窗口方法）在联合去噪窗口中对所有状态使用相同的细粒度空间粒度，导致大量与噪声相关的冗余，限制了推理效率。

Method: 1) 多尺度分块化(MSP)：对噪声更高的状态分配更粗的块，减少45%的活跃窗口token数量；2) 多尺度自注意力(MSSA)：根据查询尺度匹配可见的非汇聚键值密度，降低注意力计算成本；3) 同质噪声级别DMD(H-DMD)：从相同源噪声水平的干净预测组装假视频，减少训练与推理之间的不匹配。

Result: 在单张H200 GPU上达到22.84 FPS，比Rolling Forcing快39.6%，在短视频和长视频生成场景中均显著提升VBench分数。

Conclusion: Ms.Forcing通过噪声感知的多尺度设计，在保持静态计算图的前提下，有效平衡了流式视频生成的效率与质量，多尺度设计还帮助抵消了重叠窗口反向传播带来的额外训练成本。

Abstract: Streaming video diffusion models have made substantial progress toward interactive and dynamic world simulation, but the nested autoregressive and denoising loops of conventional next-frame generation hinder real-time deployment. Recent rolling-window methods pipeline denoising across multiple consecutive frames at different noise levels, improving throughput and long-horizon stability. However, they tokenize every state at the same fine spatial granularity, leaving substantial noise-dependent redundancy in the joint denoising window. We propose Ms.Forcing, an efficient streaming video generation paradigm that adapts spatial granularity to each state's noise level. Its Multi-Scale Patchification (MSP) assigns coarser patches to noisier states, reducing the active-window token count by 45%, while Multi-Scale Self-Attention (MSSA) matches the density of visible non-sink keys and values to each query scale to further reduce attention cost. Because both schedules are fixed by window position, Ms.Forcing retains a static, hardware-friendly computation graph. We further introduce Homogeneous-Noise-Level DMD (H-DMD), which assembles each fake video from clean predictions sharing the same source noise level, thereby reducing the mismatch between DMD training sequences and inference-time rollouts. The multi-scale design helps offset the additional training cost of backpropagating through overlapping windows. We include both quantitative and qualitative experiments to show that Ms.Forcing reaches 22.84 FPS on a single H200 GPU, 39.6% faster than Rolling Forcing, while significantly improving VBench scores in both short video and long video generation setting.

</details>


### [26] [RECO: Region-Aware Compensation for Extrinsic Perturbations in Roadside 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20947)
*Junsheng Du,Zhaocheng He,Yuhuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RECO提出了一种区域感知的外参补偿框架，通过分段6自由度姿态偏移来校正相机外参偏差，结合可学习的范围边界和可微sigmoid门控实现近远区域的平滑融合，并引入辅助重投影损失进行联合优化，在DAIR-V2X-I和Rope3D基准上显著提升了 roadside 3D检测对外参扰动的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路侧3D目标检测方法对相机外参高度敏感，即使是微小的偏差（瞬态抖动或持续漂移）也会被投影几何显著放大，导致特征错位和定位精度下降。

Method: 1）学习一个可微的范围边界将场景划分为近区和远区；2）为每个区域预测独立的6自由度姿态补偿量；3）通过可微sigmoid门控平滑融合两个补偿后的几何变换，保证连续BEV采样和稳定优化；4）引入辅助重投影损失，将3D标注投影为2D边界框与2D标注进行比较，联合标准检测目标进行优化。

Result: 在DAIR-V2X-I和Rope3D基准上，针对外参扰动（偏航角和z轴偏差）的实验中，RECO一致优于现有最先进方法；并能从瞬态扰动泛化到持续偏移，在严格标定不确定性下保持高竞争力性能。

Conclusion: RECO通过区域感知的外参补偿和联合优化策略，有效缓解了路侧3D检测中外参偏差导致的性能退化问题，具有良好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: In intelligent transportation systems, roadside 3D object detection provides wide-area perception crucial for traffic understanding, cooperative early warning, and safe autonomous driving. However, existing methods suffer from high sensitivity to camera extrinsics; even slight deviations (whether manifesting as transient jitter or persistent drift) can be significantly amplified by projective geometry. This cascade results in severe feature misalignment and degraded localization. To mitigate this limitation, we propose RECO, a region-aware extrinsic compensation framework that corrects extrinsics using piecewise 6-DoF pose offsets. RECO predicts a learnable range boundary to partition the scene into near and far regions, estimating region-specific pose corrections. A differentiable sigmoid gate then smoothly blends the two compensated geometries to preserve continuous BEV sampling and facilitate stable optimization. To supervise the refinement of extrinsics, we introduce an auxiliary reprojection loss that compares 2D bounding boxes projected from 3D ground truth against 2D annotations, optimizing it jointly with the standard detection objective. Extensive experiments on the DAIR-V2X-I and Rope3D benchmarks under extrinsic perturbations demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines across both yaw and $z$-axis deviations. RECO also generalizes from transient perturbations to persistent shifts, maintaining highly competitive performance under strict calibration uncertainty.

</details>


### [27] [FSB-Net: Frequency-Spatial Boundary Network for Brain Stroke Lesion Segmentation in Non-Contrast CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20955)
*Linke Fan,Xianglong Li,Huixin Huang,Kai Shu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FSB-Net提出了一种频域-空间边界网络，通过小波变换提取高频边界特征、交叉注意力模块增强边界以及频谱边界损失优化边界锐度，在非增强CT脑卒中病灶分割上优于U-Net、UNet++、MANet和DeepLabV3+等现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非增强CT中脑卒中病灶与正常组织对比度低、病灶形态异质（缺血性与出血性）、部分容积效应导致边界模糊，现有深度学习方法仅优化区域级重叠指标而缺乏显式边界建模，导致分割轮廓不精确，影响体积评估和治疗规划。

Method: FSB-Net包含三个核心组件：(i) 小波边界检测头（WBDH），对多尺度编码器特征应用离散小波变换，提取高频子带作为边界表示；(ii) 频域-空间交叉注意力模块（FSCAM），在小波边界特征与空间解码器特征间进行双向注意力以实现选择性边界增强；(iii) 频谱边界损失（Spectral Boundary Loss），在傅里叶域惩罚高频差异以优化边界锐度。编码器采用PVTv2-B2。

Result: 在包含缺血性和出血性病例的公开脑卒中CT数据集上，FSB-Net在平均Dice、平均IoU和HD95指标上均优于U-Net、UNet++、MANet和DeepLabV3+，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 频域分析（小波变换和傅里叶域损失）能够有效增强脑卒中病灶分割中的边界建模，弥补纯空间域区域级优化的不足，为低对比度医学图像分割提供了新思路。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of brain stroke lesions in non-contrast computed tomography (NCCT) scans is critical for rapid clinical decision-making, yet remains difficult due to the low contrast between lesion and normal brain tissue, heterogeneous lesion morphology across ischemic and hemorrhagic subtypes, and ambiguous boundaries caused by partial volume effects. Current deep learning approaches primarily optimize region-level overlap but lack explicit boundary modeling, leading to imprecise delineation that can affect volumetric assessment and treatment planning. We propose FSB-Net, a frequency-spatial boundary network that leverages frequency-domain analysis for boundary-aware stroke lesion segmentation. FSB-Net introduces three components: (i) a Wavelet Boundary Detection Head (WBDH) that applies the discrete wavelet transform to multi-scale encoder features, extracting high-frequency sub-bands as boundary representations; (ii) a Frequency-Spatial Cross-Attention Module (FSCAM) that performs bidirectional attention between wavelet boundary features and spatial decoder features for selective boundary enhancement; and (iii) a Spectral Boundary Loss that penalizes high-frequency discrepancies in the Fourier domain to optimize boundary sharpness. Built on a PVTv2-B2 encoder, FSB-Net is evaluated on a public Brain Stroke CT dataset containing both ischemic and hemorrhagic cases. Experimental results show that FSB-Net outperforms U-Net, UNet++, MANet, and DeepLabV3+ across all metrics, achieving state-of-the-art performance in mean Dice, mean IoU, and HD95.

</details>


### [28] [Unsupervised Metal Artifact Reduction in Dental CBCT using Fine-tuned Cycle-Consistent Adversarial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20977)
*G. L. T. Chamika,S. N. A. Dhanapala,P. H. S. V. Nimalaweera,Maheshi B. Dissanayake,Ruwan D. Jayasinghe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于CycleGAN的无监督深度学习框架，用于去除牙科植入物CBCT图像中的金属伪影。该方法使用约4000张非配对图像训练，集成U-Net生成器和PatchGAN判别器，在保持解剖结构完整性的同时实现伪影抑制。BRISQUE评分提升34.6%，FID从207.03降至157.04，SSIM达0.9105，推理时间仅3.03ms/层。专家验证确认高保真度，建议作为人机协同的临床决策辅助工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牙科植入物在CBCT成像中产生严重金属伪影，遮挡关键解剖结构，损害诊断精度。现有有监督方法依赖难以获得的体素对齐配对数据集，实际应用受限。

Method: 采用CycleGAN框架，使用约4000张来自ToothFairy数据集的非配对图像。生成器基于U-Net架构，判别器采用PatchGAN，专门调优以减少生成幻觉并保持形态完整性。

Result: BRISQUE评分提升34.6%，FID从207.03降至157.04，SSIM达0.9105，推理时间3.03ms/层，实现实时效率。

Conclusion: 该框架有效抑制伪影并保留解剖细节，但极端情况下可靠性有限，建议作为人机协同的临床决策辅助工具，为高保真牙科植入物成像提供可扩展的软件解决方案。

Abstract: Metal artifacts generated by dental implants significantly degrade cone-beam computed tomography (CBCT) volumes, obscuring critical anatomical structures and compromising diagnostic precision. To address this, an unsupervised deep learning framework has been proposed for Metal Artifact Reduction (MAR) utilizing a Cycle-Consistent Adversarial Network (CycleGAN) optimized for high-fidelity restoration. Unlike supervised methods that rely on unattainable voxel-aligned paired datasets, the proposed approach leverages an unpaired dataset of approximately 4,000 images, curated from the public ToothFairy dataset. The architecture integrates U-Net-based generators and PatchGAN discriminators, specifically tuned to mitigate generative hallucinations and preserve morphological integrity. Quantitative benchmarking on a held-out test set demonstrates a 34.6\% improvement in the Blind/Referenceless Image Spatial Quality Evaluator (BRISQUE) score, a substantial reduction in Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) from 207.03 to 157.04, and a superior Structural Similarity Index Measure (SSIM) of 0.9105. The framework achieves real-time efficiency with a 3.03 ms inference time per slice, effectively suppressing artifacts while preserving anatomical detail. Expert validation confirms high fidelity; however, to ensure reliability in extreme cases, the architecture is recommended as a clinical decision-support tool under human-in-the-loop oversight. By enhancing diagnostic clarity via a scalable software pipeline, this study provides a robust solution for high-fidelity dental implant imaging.

</details>


### [29] [Distribution-Alignment Bridge for Uncertainty-Aware Text-to-Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20984)
*Kyeongmo Chae,Jihoon Lee,Sangtae Ahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出分布对齐桥（DAB）框架，将文本-视频检索重新概念化为分布对齐任务，通过高斯分布建模模态不确定性，利用扩散启发的桥进行迭代精炼，在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文本-视频检索采用确定性点匹配，无法有效处理跨模态间的语义不确定性和信息不对等问题，亟需一种能够显式建模模态不确定性的概率检索框架。

Method: 1) 将文本和视频嵌入建模为高斯分布（均值+方差）；2) 设计确定性扩散启发桥，通过截断精炼过程将文本分布向视频分布迭代转换；3) 使用基于KL散度的分布感知对比损失优化跨模态相似度。

Result: 在MSR-VTT、MSVD、VATEX三个基准数据集上显著优于现有概率方法和扩散基线方法，并通过桥诱导的分布边际实现校准的不确定性感知排序。

Conclusion: DAB将概率嵌入与分布变换统一为端到端可训练系统，为文本-视频检索提供了更鲁棒且具有不确定性感知能力的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes the Distribution-Alignment Bridge (DAB), a framework that reconceptualizes text-to-video retrieval as a distribution alignment task rather than traditional deterministic point matching. By modeling both text and video embeddings as Gaussian distributions defined by mean and variance, DAB explicitly accounts for modality-specific uncertainty. We employ a deterministic, diffusion-inspired bridge to iteratively refine text distributions toward their target video distributions through a truncated refinement process. This approach unifies probabilistic embedding and distributional transformation into a cohesive, end-to-end trainable system. To optimize cross-modal similarity, we introduce a distribution-aware contrastive loss based on Kullback-Leibler divergence. Extensive evaluations on MSR-VTT, MSVD, and VATEX benchmarks confirm that DAB significantly outperforms existing probabilistic and diffusion-based baselines, while providing calibrated uncertainty-aware ranking through bridge-induced distributional margins.

</details>


### [30] [HyWorldVLA: A Vision-Language-Action Model with Hybrid World Modeling for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20988)
*Quanfu Yu,Xian Wu,Hao Xu,Liulong Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HyWorldVLA提出了一种混合世界VLA框架，通过统一像素级监督和潜在表示学习，在预训练阶段同时预测视频潜在特征和重建视频帧，在协同微调阶段仅预测潜在特征用于轨迹生成，在NAVSIM v1和v2基准上显著优于纯像素级和纯潜在级世界模型基线，并首次对自动驾驶中的世界模型噪声鲁棒性进行了全面分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素级未来预测在噪声驾驶场景下鲁棒性不足，而基于潜在的世界模型虽缓解了敏感性但缺乏可解释性和表示退化。作者旨在调和这一权衡，提出统一像素级监督和潜在表示学习的混合框架。

Method: 预训练阶段：HyWorldVLA预测预训练视频VAE编码的视频潜在特征，同时重建视频帧以提供精确的像素级监督。协同微调阶段：模型仅预测潜在特征，并将其输入动作专家网络生成轨迹。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1和v2基准上，HyWorldVLA显著优于基于像素和基于潜在的世界模型基线。首次提供了自动驾驶中世界模型噪声鲁棒性的全面定性和定量分析。

Conclusion: 混合像素级监督与潜在表示学习的方法有效调和了鲁棒性与可解释性之间的权衡，HyWorldVLA为自动驾驶中的世界模型架构设立了新基准。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models augmented with world modeling represent a promising paradigm for end-to-end autonomous driving. While pixel-level future prediction enables fine-grained spatiotemporal reasoning, it compromises robustness in noisy driving scenarios. Conversely, latent-based world models alleviate this sensitivity but often incur limited interpretability and representational degradation due to absent pixel-level grounding. To reconcile this trade-off, we propose HyWorldVLA, a hybrid world-VLA framework that unifies pixel-level supervision and latent representation learning. In the pre-training stage, HyWorldVLA predicts video latents encoded by a pre-trained video VAE, while simultaneously reconstructing video frames to provide precise pixel-level grounding. During the subsequent co-fine-tuning phase, the model exclusively predicts latent features, which are fed into an action expert to generate trajectories. Extensive experiments on NAVSIM v1 and v2 benchmarks demonstrate that HyWorldVLA significantly outperforms both pixel-based and latent-based world model baselines. Notably, we present the first comprehensive qualitative and quantitative analysis of world model noise robustness in autonomous driving, establishing a new benchmark for evaluating future architectures.

</details>


### [31] [Latent Variable-Mediated Cross-Learning for Few-Shot Acoustic Impedance Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20989)
*Junheng Peng,Yong Li,Mingwei Wang,Yi Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RD-SCL框架，通过可微分的闭式Tikhonov反褶积算子动态估计子波，结合半监督交叉学习，在仅有极少标注井数据（<1%）条件下实现高精度声阻抗成像，在SEAM和Marmousi 2基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声阻抗成像严重病态：子波未知、观测带限、标注井数据极度稀缺（<1%）。现有半监督深度学习方法要么依赖不准确的子波先验假设，要么引入辅助网络导致优化不稳定和性能下降。

Method: 提出RD-SCL框架，核心是构建一个可微分的闭式一阶Tikhonov反褶积算子，在训练过程中于频域动态估计潜在子波，无需显式辅助网络和固定子波先验；在此基础上设计对称交叉学习机制，强制标注数据与未标注数据预测之间的一致性，充分利用丰富的未标注迹。

Result: 在SEAM和Marmousi 2基准上，RD-SCL一致优于现有监督和半监督方法，以更低计算成本取得显著性能提升，仅需56.5k可学习参数，运行时竞争力强。

Conclusion: RD-SCL提供了一种实用、物理一致且高效的声阻抗成像解决方案，有效解决了少样本和子波未知问题。

Abstract: Acoustic impedance imaging is a fundamental yet severely ill-posed problem in subsurface analysis: the seismic wavelet is unknown, observations are band-limited, and labeled well-log samples are extremely scarce (typically <1% of all traces). Existing semi-supervised deep learning methods mitigate few-shot problem by incorporating forward modeling, yet they either rely on inaccurate prior wavelet assumptions or introduce auxiliary networks, leading to unstable optimization and degraded performance. We propose RD-SCL, a novel framework that integrates regularized deconvolution with semi-supervised cross-learning. At its core lies a differentiable, closed-form first-order Tikhonov deconvolution operator that dynamically estimates the latent wavelet in the frequency domain during training, providing stable physics-guided feedback without explicit auxiliary networks and fixed wavelet priors. Building on this operator, we design a symmetric cross-learning that enforces consistency between predictions on labeled and unlabeled data, thereby effectively exploiting abundant unlabeled traces. Extensive experiments on the SEAM and Marmousi 2 benchmarks demonstrate that RD-SCL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art supervised and semi-supervised methods, achieving substantial gains with lower computational cost. With only 56.5k learnable parameters and competitive runtime, RD-SCL offers a practical, physically consistent, and efficient solution for acoustic impedance imaging.

</details>


### [32] [Sparse Concept Channels in Frozen 3D CT Vision Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20993)
*Farhad Nooralahzadeh,Lea Bogensperger,Christian Bluethgen,Michael Krauthammer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出无需训练的概念通道探针（CCP）方法，发现3D医学视觉-语言模型的冻结视觉编码器中，每个放射学发现由约10个稀疏通道编码，关闭特定通道可精准消除对应发现。该方法在胸部模型（Pillar-0）和腹部模型（Merlin）上均复现，F1达0.549，BLEU达0.483，延迟降低22倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型视觉-语言模型在3D医学图像解读中表现优异，但内部编码机制不透明——不清楚哪些内部单元编码临床发现，也不清楚这些信息在表征中的具体位置。

Method: 提出无需训练的概念通道探针（CCP）方法，在冻结的视觉编码器上进行探针分析，结合语料库导出的报告模板，识别每个放射学发现对应的稀疏通道集（约10个通道）。

Result: 在胸部VLM（Pillar-0）上：稀疏通道集匹配全特征分类性能，远超零样本文本提示；关闭特定发现通道后该发现分数崩溃而无关标签保持稳定；在架构无关的腹部VLM（Merlin）上完全复现。CCP方法在临床效能和NLG指标上超越CT-CHAT（F1 0.549 vs 0.184; BLEU 0.483 vs 0.373），延迟降低22倍。

Conclusion: 冻结医学编码器对发现的表征具有稀疏性、可定位性和跨模型可迁移性，CCP方法为此提供了一种高效、无需训练的解析与生成工具。

Abstract: Large vision-language models are becoming increasingly dominant in 3D medical image interpretation, but we rarely know <i>which</i> internal units encode clinical findings or <i>where</i> that information lives in the representation. We first study this on a 3D chest vision-language model (Pillar-0) by probing its frozen vision embeddings. We show that (i) each radiological finding is encoded by a <i>sparse</i> set of ~10 vision-encoder channels that match full-feature classification performance and far exceed a zero-shot text prompting; (ii) turning off the channels tied to one finding, that finding's score collapses while unrelated labels stay stable; and (iii) the same sparse probe <i>replicates</i> on an architecturally unrelated 3D abdominal VLM (Merlin) suggesting a general property of frozen medical encoders. Our training-free concept channel probe (CCP) method, paired with a corpus-derived report template, outperforms published CT-CHAT on clinical efficacy and NLG metrics (F1 0.549 vs. 0.184; BLEU 0.483 vs. 0.373) at 22x lower latency. Our results provide a clear, reproducible characterization of how frozen medical encoders represent findings, demonstrating direct applicability across models.

</details>


### [33] [AUCH-Net: Action Unit-Based Consistency-Aware Hypergraph Network for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Facial Expression Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21004)
*Xinhan Qiu,Yan Yan,Rui Zhu,Si Chen,Hanzi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于动作单元的一致性感知超图网络（AUCH-Net），用于跨域少样本面部表情识别。该方法通过AU特征学习和视觉特征学习两个模块，利用关系一致性损失和AU正则化损失学习可迁移的特征表示，有效弥合了细粒度面部变化与高级表情类别之间的差距，在多个数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域少样本面部表情识别方法由于域差异大且目标样本有限，导致迁移特征学习效果不佳，性能不令人满意。

Method: 提出AUCH-Net，包含AU特征学习（AFL）模块和视觉特征学习（VFL）模块。AFL模块通过关系一致性损失和AU正则化损失学习AU特征；VFL模块通过关系一致性损失和分类损失学习视觉特征。模型在AU上构建一致性感知超图，建模AU与表情类别之间的连接关系。

Result: 在实验室环境和真实环境数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法一致优于多种最先进方法。

Conclusion: 建模AU之间的关系在跨域少样本面部表情识别中具有显著潜力，能够有效弥合细粒度面部变化与高级表情类别之间的鸿沟，促进可迁移特征表示的学习。

Abstract: Recently, cross-domain few-shot facial expression recognition (CF-FER) has received considerable attention. However, the performance of existing CF-FER methods is still unsatisfactory due to inferior transferable feature learning under large domain discrepancy and limited target samples. Fortunately, the action units (AUs), which indicate the movements of different facial muscles, provide consistent conceptual semantics for describing expressions within and across domains. Inspired by this, we propose a novel Action Unit-based Consistency-aware Hypergraph Network (AUCH-Net), which constructs consistency-aware hypergraphs on AUs, for CF-FER. Specifically, AUCH-Net presents a new AU feature learning (AFL) module and a new visual feature learning (VFL) module. The AFL module learns AU features under the guidance of a novel relation consistency loss and an AU regularization loss, while the VFL module learns visual features supervised by a relation consistency loss and a classification loss. By learning consistent AU features, AUCH-Net effectively models the connections between AUs and expression categories. As a result, we can bridge the gap between fine-grained facial variations and high-level expression categories, greatly facilitating the learning of transferable feature representations.Extensive experiments on both in-the-lab and in-the-wild datasets show that our method consistently outperforms several state-of-the-art methods. Our results clearly show that modeling the relationships among AUs holds significant potential for FER under cross-domain few-shot scenarios.

</details>


### [34] [Explainable Deepfake Detection Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21007)
*Abhijeet Narang,Kartik Kuckreja,Shreya Ghosh,Muhammad Haris Khan,Usman Tariq,Jianfei Cai,Abhinav Dhall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ACM Multimedia 2026的可解释深度伪造检测挑战赛基于XPlainVerse百万级基准数据集，要求参赛方法同时输出真实/伪造标签以及面向技术用户和普通用户的两种自然语言解释，并通过分类指标、语义相似度、简洁性和意图感知接地度等维度进行联合评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动深度伪造检测从简单的二分类决策向可解释系统转变，使不同背景的用户不仅能判断图像是否被篡改，还能理解为何被认为可疑。

Method: 基于XPlainVerse百万级基准数据集，要求对每张图像输出真实/伪造标签以及两种自然语言解释（详细复杂版面向技术用户、简洁简单版面向普通用户），评估结合分类指标、语义相似度、简洁性和意图感知接地度指标。

Result: 提供评估脚本、基线模型和代码（开源于GitHub），通过挑战赛推动可解释深度伪造检测方法的发展。

Conclusion: 该挑战赛将促进下一代可解释深度伪造检测器的开发，使系统同时具备分类能力和可解释性，满足实际验证场景中多样化用户的需求。

Abstract: Deepfake detection is moving beyond binary classification decisions toward systems that can also explain the visual evidence supporting those decisions. This transition is important for real-world verification settings, where diverse users need to understand not only whether an image is manipulated, but also why it is considered suspicious. The Explainable Deepfake Detection Challenge at ACM Multimedia 2026 is designed to benchmark this joint capability. Built on XPlainVerse, a million-scale benchmark for explainable deepfake detection, the challenge evaluates methods on image classification and grounded natural-language explanation generation. Participants submit a real/fake label together with two explanations for each image: a detailed complex explanation for technical users and a concise simple explanation for general users. The evaluation combines classification metrics with semantic similarity, simplicity, and intent-aware grounding metrics that assess whether explanations identify the relevant manipulated entities and supporting visual evidence. The methodologies developed through the challenge will contribute to the development of next-generation explainable deepfake detectors. Evaluation script, baseline models, and accompanying code are available on https://github.com/Abhijeet8901/XPlainVerse-ACMChallenge.

</details>


### [35] [ProCap: Prominence-guided Object Rectification for Faithful and Comprehensive Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21022)
*Debjyoti Das Adhikary,Aritra Hazra,Partha Pratim Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需重新训练模型的、基于显著性感知的迭代后处理修正框架，通过轻量级评分机制对检测到的对象进行空间显著性、时间持久性和关系动态排名，并利用迭代提示驱动循环逐步将缺失的关键对象注入字幕，在不修改原模型参数的情况下提升视频字幕的完整性和减少幻觉现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频字幕方法要么需要昂贵的重新训练大型视觉语言模型，要么现有的免训练方法仅进行单次固定修正，未区分不同对象的重要性，导致语义重要的内容被遗漏。

Method: 提出显著性感知的迭代后处理修正框架：1) 轻量级评分机制按空间显著性、时间持久性和关系动态对检测对象进行排名；2) 基于排名的迭代提示驱动精炼循环，逐步将缺失但上下文相关的对象注入字幕。

Result: 在MSVD和MSR-VTT数据集上验证，结合110人参与的人类研究及与ChatGPT/Gemini的定性比较；人类评估中，感知完整性提升高达48%，幻觉减少高达45%，且无需重新训练或参考字幕。

Conclusion: 该框架是一种轻量级、可扩展、模型无关的视频字幕改进方法，有助于提升字幕的完整性和可信度，对无障碍访问、检索等多模态理解应用具有直接价值。

Abstract: Improving video captioning quality typically demands retraining large vision-language models, an expensive and often impractical requirement. Existing training-free alternatives instead ground captions in detected objects to curb hallucination, but apply only a single, fixed correction pass without prioritizing which objects matter most, leaving semantically significant content omitted. We propose a prominence-aware, iterative post-hoc rectification framework that overcomes both limitations without modifying the underlying captioning model's parameters: a lightweight scoring mechanism ranks detected objects by spatial saliency, temporal persistence, and relational dynamics, and an iterative, prompt-driven refinement loop uses this ranking to progressively inject missing yet contextually relevant objects into the caption over multiple rounds. We validate the framework on MSVD and MSR-VTT using object-grounded automatic metrics, a 110-participant human study, and qualitative comparison against ChatGPT and Gemini; in human evaluation, the framework raises perceived completeness by up to 48% and reduces hallucination by up to 45% relative to a strong pretrained captioning baseline, all without retraining or reference captions. These results position prominence-guided iterative rectification as a lightweight, scalable, and model-agnostic route to more complete and trustworthy video captioning, with direct relevance to accessibility, retrieval, and other multimedia understanding applications.

</details>


### [36] [WAT3R: Feedforward Underwater 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21023)
*Jiayi Xu,Jiahao Lu,Ziqiang Zheng,Yihao Tan,Yaolong Zhu,Yuan Liu,Sai-Kit Yeung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WAT3R提出了一种水下3D重建的前馈框架，通过轻量级神经适应模块处理水下光照衰减和背向散射导致的视觉退化与特征不一致问题，直接从水下图像/视频中重建像素对齐的3D点图和相机位姿，在FLSea、SQUID和USOD10K数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下光照衰减和背向散射导致视觉质量下降、跨视角特征一致性被破坏，使得现有前馈多视图3D重建方法在水下场景中表现不佳。

Method: 提出WAT3R框架，将退化适应建模为几何约束过程，集成轻量级神经适应模块来灵活应对水下成像效应，通过单次前向传递直接从水下视频输出像素对齐的3D点图和相机位姿。

Result: 在FLSea、SQUID和USOD10K数据集上，WAT3R在多视图/单目深度估计和相机位姿估计等3D重建任务中一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: WAT3R通过退化适应策略有效解决了水下成像退化对3D重建的影响，实现了高效、高质量的水下3D重建。

Abstract: Reliable feedforward underwater 3D reconstruction remains challenging due to severe light attenuation and backscattering, which degrade visual quality and disrupt feature consistency across views, leading to inaccurate multi-view geometry. To address this issue, we propose WAT3R, a feed-forward framework for reconstructing 3D scenes directly from underwater images. By leveraging degradation adaptation as a geometry-constrained process, WAT3R integrates a lightweight neural adaptation module to flexibly account for these underwater imaging effects, thereby improving multi-view reconstruction quality. Implemented in a single forward pass, WAT3R directly and efficiently outputs pixel-aligned 3D point maps and camera poses from underwater videos, allowing a high-quality underwater 3D reconstruction. Experiments conducted on the FLSea, SQUID, and USOD10K datasets show that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art approaches on 3D reconstruction tasks, including multi-view/monocular depth estimation and camera pose estimation.

</details>


### [37] [GroupVideo: Multi-Identity Customized Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21027)
*Xinyang Song,Libin Wang,Jianxin Sun,Qi Li,Dandan Zheng,JingDong Chen,Zhenan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GroupVideo提出了一个基于视频扩散Transformer的多人物身份定制视频生成框架，通过多模态身份对齐（视觉对齐+语义对齐）和ID定位模块（空间引导、边界框约束、掩码正则化）来解决多身份场景下的身份混淆和“复制粘贴”问题，并构建了2万视频的高质量多ID数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有身份定制视频生成方法仅适用于单身份场景，多身份场景下缺乏显式身份分离机制导致身份混淆；直接扩展单身份框架拼接人脸图像作为输入会导致不自然的表情和动作（“复制粘贴”现象）。

Method: 基于视频扩散Transformer架构，包含三个核心模块：(1) 多模态身份对齐：视觉对齐联合编码多个面部图像提供身份参考，语义对齐引入语义感知器增强动作自然性；(2) ID定位模块：通过空间引导、边界框约束和掩码正则化损失解决身份混合问题，提升身份保真度和训练效率；(3) 构建了包含2万视频的高质量多ID数据集。

Result: 大量实验表明GroupVideo在生成具有一致身份和自然动作的多角色视频方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: GroupVideo通过多模态身份对齐和ID定位模块有效解决了多身份视频生成中的身份混淆和动作不自然问题，构建的数据集为未来研究提供了重要资源。

Abstract: Current identity customized video generation methodologies are predominantly limited to single-identity scenarios, as the lack of explicit identity separation mechanisms often leads to identity confusion in multi-identity settings. Existing multi-identity approaches, which directly extend single-identity frameworks by concatenating face images as input conditions, frequently result in unnatural facial expressions and motions, manifesting as the "copy-paste" phenomenon. To overcome these limitations, we introduce GroupVideo, a novel framework that leverages multiple individual photographs to generate identitycustomized video. Built upon Video Diffusion Transformers, GroupVideo incorporates multimodal identity alignment: visual alignment jointly encodes multiple face images to provide robust identity references, while semantic alignment introduces a semantic perceiver to enhance the naturalness of motions. An ID localization module with spatial guidance is introduced to address identity blending and enhance identity fidelity, along with bounding box constraints and mask regularization loss, to focus on facial regions and improve training efficiency. In response to the shortage of multi-ID video datasets, we have curated a comprehensive high-quality dataset of 20,000 videos, thereby establishing a crucial resource to advance future research in multi-ID video generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GroupVideo outperforms existing methods in generating multi-character videos with consistent identities and natural motions.

</details>


### [38] [Spectral-Spatial Synergistic Guided Network for Hyperspectral Salient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21032)
*Yanyan Peng,Tingfa Xu,Yao Xiao,Peifu Liu,Shuyan Bai,Fengxiang Xu,Jianan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出S3GNet，一种轻量级的高光谱显著性目标检测网络，通过谱-空协同引导和结构感知核心，利用谱导数与区域层次建模提取材料本征特征，结合流间全局交互与流内空间引导的注意力机制，以及渐进式门控精化解码器，在计算效率和检测精度上均超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高光谱显著性目标检测方法常误解数据本质，将光照等外部因素引起的偶然光谱变化与目标本身材料属性引起的本质光谱差异混淆，导致表征脆弱、预测含噪。

Method: 提出S3GNet，包含三个核心模块：1) 参数无关的频谱结构感知模块（利用谱导数与区域层次建模提取对光照鲁棒的本征特征）；2) 流感知注意力模块（通过流间全局交互与流内空间引导实现谱-空协同）；3) 渐进式门控精化解码器（通过多尺度特征最优集成恢复精确边界与细节）。

Result: 实验表明S3GNet在计算效率和检测精度上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过结构感知为核心的闭环信息流设计（鲁棒光谱建模、跨流协同感知、多尺度精化解码），有效解决了高光谱显著性检测中光照变化导致的误判问题。

Abstract: Hyperspectral salient object detection aims to identify visually salient regions from hyperspectral images. Existing methods often fail because they fundamentally misunderstand the data, confusing incidental spectral variations caused by external factors such as illumination with essential spectral differences caused by the intrinsic material properties of the object. This leads to fragile representations and noisy predictions. To this end, we propose a lightweight and efficient Spectral-Spatial Synergistic Guided Network (S3GNet), with structure perception as the core, to build a closed-loop information flow around spectrum robust modeling, cross-stream co-perception and multi-scale refinement decoding. S3GNet introduces a parameter-free Spectral Structure-Aware Module that leverages spectral derivatives and regional hierarchical modeling to extract intrinsic features of robustness against illumination variations. Our Stream-Aware Attention Module achieves effective spectral-spatial collaboration through inter-stream global interaction and intra-stream spatial guidance. Furthermore, a Progressive Gated Refinement Decoder ensures precise object boundaries and detail recovery by optimally integrating multi-scale features. Experimental results show that S3GNet achieves superior performance in both computational efficiency and detection accuracy compared to existing methods.

</details>


### [39] [GeoThreat: Transferable Targeted Adversarial Attacks on Large Vision-Language Models for Remote Sensing Image Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21036)
*Yimin Fu,Yuefeng Bai,Baicheng Pan,Zhunga Liu,Michael K. Ng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoThreat，一种针对遥感图像解释中大型视觉-语言模型的可迁移定向对抗攻击方法，通过在概念层和感知层联合调制对抗表示，实现黑盒设置下指定语义响应的跨模态操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗攻击研究主要针对自然图像的一般视觉-语言任务，遥感领域的相关探索严重不足。遥感图像理解需要对局部判别线索和全局场景上下文进行联合推理，这给在黑盒设置下实现向指定响应的可迁移语义操控带来了额外挑战。

Method: GeoThreat在概念层和感知层双层面调制对抗表示：概念表示利用替代图像编码器的类别令牌，感知表示通过协作重要性估计从对抗样本的补丁令牌中提取。引入对抗-目标相似度梯度更准确刻画局部视觉线索对语义操控的贡献，并通过跨注意力机制将感知表示与目标补丁令牌动态对齐。最后通过概念校准和感知适应的集成联合优化迭代更新对抗扰动。

Result: 在多种大型视觉-语言模型上的大量实验表明，GeoThreat在可迁移性和可控性方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: GeoThreat有效解决了遥感场景下针对LVLM的可迁移定向对抗攻击难题，通过概念-感知双层次调制实现了更强的语义操控能力。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks against large vision-language models (LVLMs) serve as an effective means of assessing their robustness in cross-modal semantic understanding. Existing studies mainly focus on corrupting visual inputs to induce predefined erroneous responses in general vision-language tasks, whereas corresponding investigations in remote sensing fields remain largely underexplored. Compared with natural image understanding, remote sensing image interpretation requires joint reasoning over local discriminative cues and global scene context. This poses additional challenges to achieving transferable semantic manipulation toward specified responses under black-box settings. To tackle these challenges, we propose GeoThreat, a transferable targeted adversarial attack method against LVLMs for remote sensing image interpretation. Specifically, GeoThreat modulates adversarial representations in accordance with the target content at both conceptual and perceptual levels. The class tokens from surrogate image encoders are employed as conceptual representations, while perceptual representations are distilled from patch tokens of the adversarial example through collaborative importance estimation. Beyond merely rolling out attention scores across layers, we incorporate adversarial-target similarity gradients to more faithfully characterize the relevance of local visual cues to the intended semantic manipulation. The perceptual representations are then dynamically aligned with target patch tokens in a cross-attentive manner, facilitating the adaptation of local cues toward designated semantic details. Finally, adversarial perturbations are iteratively updated via ensemble-based joint optimization of conceptual calibration and perceptual adaptation. Extensive experiments across diverse LVLMs demonstrate the superiority of GeoThreat in both transferability and controllability.

</details>


### [40] [HyperImageNet: A Large-Scale High-Spatial Resolution Hyperspectral Imagery Classification Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21050)
*Chuguang Zeng,Jingtao Li,Yinhe Liu,Yanfei Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了HyperImageNet——一个包含26,084张高光谱影像、224个光谱波段和138个精细土地覆盖类别的大规模基准数据集，支持语义分割和实例分割，并建立了开放环境基准来评估代表性方法和HyperFree基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高光谱数据集缺乏大规模、细粒度标注以及同时支持语义和实例分割的能力，难以推动精细级高光谱土地覆盖理解研究的发展。

Method: 从机载高光谱影像中收集26,084个影像块，提供原始图像、像素级语义标签和对象级实例掩码，并建立具有严格空间分离的开放环境基准。

Result: 实验验证了HyperImageNet在细粒度高光谱理解中的有效性，并展示了其在开放环境遥感研究中的能力。

Conclusion: HyperImageNet为高光谱遥感领域提供了一个大规模、细粒度、支持多任务的基准数据集，推动了开放环境下精细土地覆盖理解的研究。

Abstract: We present HyperImageNet, a large-scale benchmark for fine-grained hyperspectral land-cover understanding. The dataset contains 26,084 airborne hyperspectral image patches with 224 spectral bands and 138 fine-grained land-cover categories. Unlike existing datasets, HyperImageNet provides raw imagery, pixel-level semantic labels, and object-level instance masks, supporting both semantic and instance segmentation. Furthermore, we establish an open-environment benchmark with strict spatial separation to evaluate representative methods and the HyperFree foundation model. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of HyperImageNet for fine-grained hyperspectral understanding and open-environment remote sensing research.

</details>


### [41] [Achieving Text-based Person Retrieval with Any Granularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21057)
*Jialong Zuo,Hanyu Zhou,Dongyue Wu,Yongtai Deng,Mengdan Tan,Nong Sang,Changxin Gao,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对文本描述行人检索中查询粒度不确定性的问题，提出了任意粒度文本行人检索的新范式，构建了五级粒度数据集UFine6926-MG、多粒度评估基准MG-Eval以及跨模态多粒度对齐与匹配框架CMAM，实验显示CMAM在所有粒度级别上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本行人检索方法忽略了真实场景中查询粒度固有的不确定性——用户可能使用粗略描述（如"穿红衣服的人"）或精细描述（如"穿红色耐克T恤、蓝色牛仔裤、白色运动鞋的年轻男性"），而现有方法仅针对固定粒度设计，无法灵活处理多变粒度查询。

Method: 1) 形式化定义五级粒度谱系（从粗到细）；2) 通过多粒度文本标注引擎构建UFine6926-MG多粒度数据集；3) 提出MG-Eval综合评估基准，包含渐进式精细文本和跨身份标签；4) 提出CMAM框架，包含正交专家感知（解耦粒度特定特征）、概率对齐（建模查询不确定性下的多对多匹配）和粒度一致推理（通过跨模态粒度验证引导特征学习）。

Result: CMAM框架在所有粒度级别上显著优于最先进的现有方法，验证了其对查询粒度不确定性的有效处理能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为更实用的行人检索系统建立了基础基准和稳健基线，解决了查询粒度不确定性的关键问题，推动文本行人检索向实际应用迈进。

Abstract: Text-based person retrieval faces a critical but under-explored challenge: the inherent uncertainty of query granularity in real-world scenarios. This paper introduces a new paradigm, Text-based Person Retrieval with Any Granularity, and provides a systematic solution. First, we formalize a five-level granularity spectrum and construct UFine6926-MG, a high-quality multi-grained dataset annotated comprehensively at all granularities via a novel Multi-grained Text Annotation Engine. Second, acknowledging that coarse queries naturally correspond to multiple valid candidates, we propose MG-Eval, a holistic evaluation benchmark with progressively detailed texts and cross-identity labels that reflect real-world semantics, alongside tailored evaluation metrics and protocols. Third, after a comprehensive diagnosis reveals the systemic limitations of existing research, we propose the Cross-modal Multi-grained Aligning and Matching (CMAM) framework. CMAM achieves granularity-aware retrieval through: 1) orthogonal-expert perception to disentangle granularity-specific features; 2) probabilistic alignment to model many-to-many matches under query uncertainty; and 3) granularity-consistent reasoning to steer feature learning via joint cross-modal granularity verification. Experiments demonstrate that CMAM significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods across all granularity levels. This work establishes a foundational benchmark and a robust baseline, paving the way for more practical person retrieval systems.

</details>


### [42] [MVEI & EmObserver: Empowering MLLM-Oriented Visual Emotional Intelligence via Emotion Statement Judgement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21061)
*Daiqing Wu,Dongbao Yang,Jiashu Yao,Hongrui Zhang,Can Ma,Yu Zhou,Sicheng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在视觉情感分析（AICA）领域缺乏系统评估的问题，提出情感陈述判断（ESJ）范式，构建大规模数据集INSETS-462k和精细化基准MVEI，并训练情感导向模型EmObserver，实验表明该方案在准确性、泛化性和推理忠实度上均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在视觉情感智能方面缺乏系统性评估，原因在于传统AICA范式与MLLMs开放式、指令驱动的特性之间存在结构性不匹配，具体表现为：缺乏合理响应选项、情感分类体系有限、忽视上下文因素、以及标注成本高昂。

Method: 提出情感陈述判断（ESJ）范式，将输入保持开放性表达，输出约束为判别性判断；构建INSETS-462k数据集和MVEI基准（覆盖情感极性、情感解读、场景上下文、感知主观性）；并基于ESJ通过多阶段训练策略优化EmObserver模型。

Result: 在MVEI上对多种MLLMs进行广泛评估，揭示了当前人工视觉情感智能的细粒度洞察；在多个AICA基准上，EmObserver在准确性、泛化性和推理忠实度方面均表现优异。

Conclusion: ESJ是一种实用的范式，MVEI是一个全面的基准，EmObserver为推动面向MLLMs的视觉情感智能提供了先进的基线方案。

Abstract: Affective Image Content Analysis (AICA) aims to recognize and understand emotions elicited by visual content, representing an indispensable step toward Artificial General Intelligence (AGI). However, despite the rapid progress of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), systematic evaluation of their visual emotional intelligence remains largely absent from recent model releases. We attribute this gap to a structural mismatch between conventional AICA paradigms and the open-ended, instruction-driven nature of MLLMs, where further analysis reveals four major limitations: omission of plausible responses, limited emotion taxonomies, neglect of contextual factors, and labor-intensive annotation. To overcome these barriers, we introduce Emotion Statement Judgement (ESJ), a statement-verification formulation that preserves the expressiveness of the input space while constraining outputs to discriminative judgements. We further develop INSETS, a labor-efficient pipeline that instantiates ESJ at scale by constructing INSETS-462k and supporting MVEI, a rigorously refined benchmark spanning sentiment polarity, emotion interpretation, scene context, and perception subjectivity. Beyond evaluation, we build EmObserver, an emotion-oriented MLLM optimized on ESJ through an elaborate multi-stage recipe. Extensive evaluation of broad-spectrum MLLMs on MVEI reveals fine-grained insights into current artificial visual emotional intelligence, while experiments on multiple AICA benchmarks demonstrate the accuracy, generalization, and reasoning faithfulness of EmObserver. Collectively, these results establish ESJ as a practical formulation, MVEI as a comprehensive benchmark, and EmObserver as an advanced baseline for advancing MLLM-oriented visual emotional intelligence. Code will be released at: https://github.com/wdqqdw/EmObserver.

</details>

*Chengyang Zhang,Wenchuan Zhang,Bo Li,Xinyu Liu,Jiaming Yang,Mengran Li,Chenxun Deng,Jie Chen,Yang Zhang,Wei Ju,Yuhao Yi,Hong Bu,Jiancheng Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出当前病理视觉语言模型评估中存在的三个被忽视的问题：视觉输入非必要、领域训练提升准确率但未成比例提升视觉绑定、实体级注意力分散且缺乏查询特异性。为此提出PathBind基准（含2600个样本），评估18个VLM后发现当前模型在答案性能与视觉-语义绑定之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理VLM评估过度依赖VQA准确率，忽视了模型是否真正依赖视觉信息进行推理。作者发现三个关键问题：1) 无视觉输入时模型仍能取得较高准确率；2) 领域训练提升准确率但未真正增强视觉绑定能力；3) 模型注意力机制在实体级层面缺乏特异性。这些问题导致对病理VLM多模态能力的严重误判。

Method: 提出PathBind基准，包含三个子集：PathBind-VQA（1500个问题，覆盖6个维度）、PathBind-PTA（600个问题，来自私有病理教学图谱）、PathBind-Grounding（500个专家标注的区域级样本）。每个子集经过任务特定的自动化过滤和专家审查以减少文本捷径、增强实体-区域对应。在18个VLM上进行VQA评估，并在10个VLM上进行Grounding评估。

Result: Gemini-3-Pro在5个VQA基准上无视觉输入时仍达53.5%平均准确率；相比Qwen2.5-VL-7B，Patho-R1-7B多模态增益低5.8个百分点、注意力IoU低3.7个百分点；在PathVG上，不同实体查询的注意力图高度相关。当前病理VLM在答案性能与视觉-语义绑定之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 现有病理VLM评估存在严重偏差，仅靠VQA准确率无法反映模型真实的多模态能力。PathBind基准能够更全面地评估模型的视觉-语义绑定能力，揭示了当前模型在视觉理解方面的实质性不足。

Abstract: Pathology vision-language models (VLMs) have recently progressed rapidly and are commonly evaluated by answer accuracy on pathology VQA benchmarks. However, we dig into current evaluations and identify three overlooked issues: 1) Visual evidence is not always necessary. For instance, Gemini-3-Pro achieves 53.5% average accuracy across 5 VQA benchmarks without any visual input. 2) Domain training can improve accuracy without proportional gains in visual binding. Compared with Qwen2.5-VL-7B, Patho-R1-7B exhibits a 5.8-point lower multimodal gain and a 3.7-point lower attention IoU. 3) Entity-level attention is diffuse and weakly query-specific. On PathVG, attention maps remain highly correlated across different entity queries. These issues can lead to substantial misjudgments of pathology VLMs' actual multimodal capabilities. To this end, we present PathBind, a benchmark comprising 2,600 samples: PathBind-VQA with 1,500 questions across six dimensions, PathBind-PTA with 600 questions from a private pathology teaching atlas, and PathBind-Grounding with 500 expert-curated region-level samples. Each component undergoes task-specific automated filtering and expert review to reduce textual shortcuts and improve entity-region correspondence. We evaluate 18 representative VLMs on VQA samples of PathBind and five existing pathology VQA benchmarks, and further evaluate 10 VLMs on PathBind-Grounding and PathVG. Results show that current pathology VLMs still exhibit a substantial gap between answer-side performance and visual-semantic binding.

</details>


### [44] [TransBiolab: A Real-World Multi-View Dataset of Cluttered Transparent Biomedical Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21071)
*Ke Ma,Yifei Wang,Meng Wang,Tian Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TrainsBiolab是一个面向透明生物医学塑料器皿的RGB-D数据集，包含161,315帧、98个场景、1.03M实例标注，涵盖15种实验室器物的6D位姿、遮罩、深度及相机校准，并定义了分割、深度估计和6D位姿估计的基准测试及机器人操作评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有透明物体数据集缺乏对多目标杂乱场景、遮挡和多视角校准的综合评估，无法满足自主生物医学实验室中透明塑料器皿的视觉感知需求。

Method: 构建多视角校准的真实世界RGB-D数据集，沿三个难度轴（物品种类、数量、视角）组织数据，定义分割、深度估计与补全、6D位姿估计基准，并进行系统级机器人操作评估。

Result: 数据集包含161,315帧、98场景、1.03M实例标注、15种实验室器物类型的6D位姿、完整/可见遮罩、深度和每帧相机校准。

Conclusion: 该数据集为透明重复实例、杂乱场景和多视角实验室环境下的分割、深度估计、6D位姿估计和多视角推理提供了关键资源，填补了现有数据集在多目标杂乱遮挡和校准多视角方面的空白。

Abstract: Autonomous biomedical laboratories increasingly rely on visual perception to recognize, localize, and manipulate transparent plasticware, yet high-quality real-world datasets for this setting remain limited. The scarcity of domain-relevant data is particularly restrictive in cluttered multi-object scenes, where mutual occlusion and view-dependent appearance changes remain challenging even for contemporary visual foundation models. Existing transparent-object datasets have advanced segmentation, depth, and pose estimation, but they usually do not evaluate the combined setting of multi-object clutter, occlusion, and calibrated multi-view capture that characterizes real laboratory manipulation scenes. To address this gap, we present TrainsBiolab, a real-world RGB-D dataset of cluttered transparent biomedical objects captured as calibrated multi-view sequences. TrainsBiolab contains 161,315 frames from 98 scenes and 1.03M instance annotations over 15 laboratory object types, including 6D poses, full and visible masks, depth, and per-frame camera calibration. The dataset is organized along three axes that reflect operational difficulty: object category, the total number of objects in a frame, and camera viewpoint. We further define dataset-centric benchmarks for segmentation, depth estimation and completion, and 6D pose estimation, and report a system-level robot manipulation evaluation enabled by the released annotations and calibrations. By focusing on repeated transparent instances, clutter, and multi-view laboratory capture, TrainsBiolab provides a resource for segmentation, depth estimation, 6D pose estimation, and multi-view reasoning in autonomous laboratory manipulation. Project page: https://dualtransparency.github.io/TransBiolab/.

</details>


### [45] [Show, Don't Tell: Evaluating Spatial Cognition in Generative Pixels Rather Than LLM Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21072)
*Xu Wang,Kaixiang Yao,Miao Pan,Xiaohe Zhou,Xuanyu Liu,Wenqi Zhang,Xuhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ProVisE框架，通过协议约束的视觉答案让图像生成模型参与空间推理评估，并构建SpatialGen-Bench基准，发现图像生成模型在像素空间表达上有优势，而文本输出VLM在组合空间推理上更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间推理基准通常要求坐标、选项或文本作为答案，与图像生成模型在像素空间中直接外化空间判断的能力不匹配，导致无法在同一任务语义下公平评估图像生成模型和文本输出VLM。

Method: 提出ProVisE框架，包含协议约束的视觉答案生成和解析流程，以及Agentic Builder用于自动构建和验证任务特定协议；同时构建SpatialGen-Bench诊断基准，包含470个样本、14个空间子任务、4个能力层级和多种答案形式。

Result: 图像生成模型在可直接在像素空间外化空间答案的任务上具有竞争力；文本输出VLM在组合空间推理上保持明显优势。

Conclusion: 像素空间表达和文本推理具有互补优势，ProVisE为研究图像生成模型的空间认知建立了与原始指标兼容的测试平台。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence is essential for agents to move from static semantic understanding toward interacting with the physical world. Many spatial tasks are grounded in continuous visual scenes, where locations, regions, and paths are more naturally expressed by pointing, marking, or drawing than by reporting precise coordinates or discrete textual symbols. Yet existing spatial reasoning benchmarks usually require coordinates, options, or text, creating an answer-interface mismatch for image-generation models. This makes it difficult to evaluate image-generation models under the same task semantics as text-output VLMs, despite their ability to externalize spatial judgments directly in pixel space. We propose ProVisE (Protocolized Visual Evaluation), a benchmark-agnostic framework that elicits protocol-constrained visual answers from image-generation models and parses them into structured predictions compatible with original metrics. ProVisE also includes an Agentic builder that constructs and validates task-specific protocols for new benchmarks. We further introduce SpatialGen-Bench, a curated diagnostic benchmark of 470 samples across 14 spatial subtasks, four capability levels, and diverse answer forms. We evaluate representative text-output VLMs and image-generation models in a unified setting and validate Agentic protocol construction on six external spatial benchmarks. Results show that image-generation models are competitive when spatial answers can be externalized directly in pixel space, while text-output VLMs retain a clear advantage in compositional spatial reasoning. These findings reveal complementary strengths of pixel-space expression and text-based reasoning and establish a metric-compatible testbed for studying spatial cognition in image-generation models.

</details>


### [46] [C-PTQ: Fisher-weighted Channel-wise Sensitivity for Post-training Quantization of MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21076)
*Jiameng Li,Han Zhou,Matthew B. Blaschko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出C-PTQ，一种统一的后训练量化方法，通过Fisher加权目标近似Hessian矩阵，将任务敏感性注入通道缩放过程，在不使用额外模块的情况下提升多模态大模型的量化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型（MLLMs）部署受限于高内存和计算成本。现有PTQ方法在量化时存在异常通道导致性能下降的问题，且已有的通道缩放方法使用模态级或令牌级指标，无法捕捉通道对任务特定损失的影响，导致重要性因子与缩放因子不对齐，性能次优。

Method: 提出C-PTQ，一种统一的通道级后训练量化方法。受二阶导数启发，设计Fisher加权目标作为可处理的Hessian近似，将任务敏感性无缝注入到通道缩放（CWS）过程中，统一任务损失扰动与量化误差。

Result: 在Qwen2.5VL、InternVL2和LLaVA-OV三个模型上，覆盖8个基准测试，在仅权重量化和权重-激活量化两种设置下均达到最先进性能，且无需LoRA等辅助模块。

Conclusion: C-PTQ通过Fisher加权目标有效对齐通道重要性与缩放因子，在保持高效率的同时显著提升量化后多模态大模型的性能，验证了二阶信息在PTQ中的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) require huge memory and computational costs, which limits their practical deployment. Post-training quantization (PTQ) techniques offer an efficient solution for model compression and inference acceleration. Yet, the quantized model faces performance degradation due to outlier channels, which are highly sensitive to quantization and substantially impair activation fidelity and task accuracy. To protect these salient channels during quantization, existing PTQ methods leverage modality- or token-level metrics to guide channel-wise scaling (CWS) of LLM decoders. However, these orthogonal measurements fail to capture channel-wise impacts on task-specific loss, and the misalignment between importance and scaling factors ultimately leads to suboptimal performance. To address this issue, we propose C-PTQ, a unified channel-wise PTQ method that harmonizes task-specific loss perturbation and quantization error. Motivated by second-order derivatives, we design a Fisher-weighted objective as a tractable Hessian approximation, seamlessly injecting task sensitivity into the scaling process. Notably, we achieve state-of-the-art performance without auxiliary modules like LoRA, thereby maintaining high efficiency. Experiments on Qwen2.5VL, InternVL2 and LLaVA-OV across 8 benchmarks demonstrate our effectiveness in both weight-only and weight-activation settings.

</details>


### [47] [The RealDefocus Benchmark for Defocus Deblurring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21078)
*Tim Seizinger,Zhuyun Zhou,Radu Timofte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了RealDefocus基准数据集，它基于真实世界的RealBokeh数据集构建，用于单图像散焦去模糊(SIDD)任务。该基准提供了配对的散焦输入和清晰真值图像、预定义的训练/验证/测试划分、统一的评估框架，以及跨数据集验证的基准测试协议，旨在解决SIDD领域因缺乏真实高分辨率数据集和标准化协议导致的评估困难问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SIDD领域的评估因缺乏真实的高分辨率数据集、良好对齐的散焦/清晰图像对以及标准化的评估协议而面临挑战，导致评估结果难以复现和比较。

Method: 基于真实世界的RealBokeh数据集构建RealDefocus基准，提供配对的散焦输入和清晰真值图像、预定义的训练/验证/测试划分、统一的评估框架，并设计了包含跨数据集验证的基准测试协议。

Result: 构建了RealDefocus基准数据集与评估框架，支持图像恢复方法和神经渲染方法的比较，并提供了跨数据集验证的标准化评估流程。

Conclusion: RealDefocus通过提供真实高分辨率数据、标准化协议和统一评估框架，有望推动SIDD领域严格且可复现的评估。

Abstract: Single-Image Defocus Deblurring (SIDD) aims to recover an all-in-focus image from a single defocused observation, but rigorous and reproducible evaluation remains challenging due to the scarcity of realistic, high-resolution datasets with well-aligned defocused/sharp pairs and standardized protocols. We build on RealDefocus, a benchmark derived from the real-world RealBokeh dataset originally proposed for Bokeh Rendering. RealDefocus provides paired defocused inputs and sharp ground truth images, predefined training/validation/test splits, and a unified evaluation framework for comparing image restoration and neural rendering approaches. We further outline a benchmarking protocol with cross-dataset validation to assess reconstruction quality and generalization. The project page is publicly available at: www.github.com/TimSeizinger/RealDefocus-Benchmark.

</details>


### [48] [Geo3R: Mitigating Spatial Reasoning Hallucination in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21085)
*Mingyu Wang,Weilin Jin,Wenbo Li,Haoyang Huang,Tong Jia,Ying Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Geo3R，一个无需训练、即插即用的框架，通过引入几何证据和结构化3D推理来缓解多模态大模型在空间关系推理中的幻觉问题，在18个任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在空间关系推理中容易产生与真实3D场景矛盾的幻觉，而现有缓解方法由于无法弥补2D视觉表征与3D空间现实之间的根本差距，效果有限。

Method: 提出Geo3R，一个无需训练、即插即用的框架，通过融入几何证据和结构化3D推理来缓解空间推理幻觉。

Result: 在三个基准测试的18个任务上，Geo3R显著减少了多种MLLMs的空间推理幻觉，无需额外训练，超越了现有模型和方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入3D几何推理可以有效弥合2D视觉与3D空间之间的鸿沟，是缓解空间推理幻觉的有效途径。

Abstract: Despite remarkable progress in visual understanding, Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) remain prone to hallucinations when reasoning about spatial relationships, often producing judgments that contradict the true 3D structure of the scene. Though several existing works have proposed to mitigate hallucinations, our analysis indicates that they show limited effectiveness in spatial reasoning, as they fail to bridge the fundamental gap between 2D visual representations and 3D spatial reality. Based on this finding, we define hallucinations arising from insufficient spatial structure modeling as spatial reasoning hallucination, a subcategory of relation hallucination that existing mitigation methods fail to address. We further identify three typical scenarios where such hallucinations frequently occur: perspective effects, object orientation, and viewpoint changes. To this end, we propose Geo3R, a training-free, plug-and-play framework that incorporates geometric evidence and structured 3D reasoning to mitigate spatial reasoning hallucination. Experiments on three benchmarks, covering 18 tasks across all three scenarios, show that Geo3R substantially reduces spatial reasoning hallucination across diverse MLLMs without additional training, outperforming existing models and methods.

</details>


### [49] [Loss Landscape Topology Reveals Why Simple Baselines are Competitive at 3D Point Cloud Segmentation Under Class Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21089)
*Antonis Savva,Christos Kyrkou,Theocharis Theocharides*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了11种类别不平衡处理方法在3D点云语义分割中的效果，发现在极端(641:1)和中等(56:1)不平衡比例下，标准交叉熵损失与专门方法性能差距仅0.8-3.3% mIoU。通过多层面机制分析，揭示了不平衡程度塑造了优化景观的拓扑结构（极端不平衡导致窄解盆地，中等不平衡形成平坦高原），限制了损失层面修改的有效性。这是首个对2D有效方法在3D点云分割中迁移失效机制的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D点云语义分割面临严重的类别不平衡问题，但2D视觉中专门处理不平衡的方法在3D场景下的有效性尚不明确，需要系统的评估和机制性解释。

Method: 在极端(641:1)和中等(56:1)不平衡比例的数据集上，对11种不平衡处理方法进行系统评估；通过错误模式、决策边界和优化景观几何的多层面机制分析，揭示损失层面修改有效性的几何约束。

Result: 标准交叉熵在各类架构和数据集上表现竞争力强，与专门方法差距仅0.8-3.3% mIoU；不平衡程度塑造优化景观拓扑，极端不平衡导致窄解盆地，中等不平衡形成平坦高原；专门方法若调优不当可能显著退化。

Conclusion: 标准交叉熵是稳健基线，专门方法改进有限且依赖调优；首次从优化景观几何角度解释了2D有效方法在3D点云分割中迁移失效的机制原因。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation of 3D point clouds faces severe class imbalance, yet the effectiveness of specialized imbalance-aware methods from 2D computer vision remains unclear in 3D contexts. We systematically evaluate 11 imbalance mitigation approaches across datasets with extreme (641:1) and moderate (56:1) imbalance ratios, revealing a surprising finding: standard cross-entropy with uniform weighting achieves competitive performance, typically within 0.8-3.3% mIoU of specialized methods across architectures and datasets. Through multifaceted mechanistic analysis of error patterns, decision boundaries, and the geometry of the optimization landscape, our analyses suggest that imbalance severity shapes the topology, creating narrow solution basins under extreme imbalance and flat plateaus under moderate imbalance. This appears to constrain the effectiveness of loss-level modifications, as all methods must navigate these geometric constraints. Our findings offer practical guidance; standard cross-entropy provides a robust baseline, with specialized methods offering modest improvements (0.8-3.3% mIoU) that vary by architecture and dataset but risk substantial degradation if poorly tuned. This work provides the first mechanistic explanation for why techniques proven effective in 2D do not readily transfer to point-based 3D point cloud segmentation, validated across two representative architectures.

</details>


### [50] [HalluScope: Fine-grained Hallucination Diagnosis for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21105)
*Weilin Jin,Mingyu Wang,Wenbo Li,Haoyang Huang,Yifan Wu,Ying Li,Gang Huang,Zhonghai Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向多模态大语言模型的细粒度幻觉诊断任务，联合进行幻觉检测、分类和可解释性解释生成。通过构建 HalluScope-30K 数据集和多粒度联合奖励函数训练 HalluScope 模型，实现当前最优性能，并能有效指导目标模型纠正幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 幻觉研究主要依赖粗粒度检测方法，难以提供足够诊断信息以理解幻觉类型并支持下游幻觉缓解。

Method: 1) 提出细粒度幻觉诊断统一任务（检测+分类+可解释性解释生成）；2) 构建自动化数据生成管道并产出 HalluScope-30K 数据集（覆盖8种来源、5类任务）；3) 设计多粒度联合奖励函数，训练 HalluScope-4B 和 HalluScope-8B 模型。

Result: 在 MHALO 基准和细粒度幻觉分类基准上达到最先进性能；检测与分类在联合优化下相互促进；诊断驱动的反馈实验表明细粒度诊断解释能有效指导 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 和 LLaVA-1.5-7B 纠正幻觉，完整诊断显著优于所有基线。

Conclusion: 细粒度幻觉诊断对理解和缓解 MLLM 幻觉具有重要价值，检测与分类的联合优化以及可解释诊断反馈能有效提升模型可靠性。

Abstract: Although Multimodal Large Language Models have achieved strong performance across a wide range of vision-language tasks, they still suffer from hallucinations, where model outputs become inconsistent with the visual content, textual context, or commonsense knowledge. Existing studies primarily address this problem through coarse-grained detection. However, these approaches often provide insufficient diagnostic information for understanding hallucination types and supporting downstream hallucination mitigation. To bridge this gap, we propose fine-grained hallucination diagnosis for MLLMs, a new unified task that jointly performs hallucination detection, classification, and interpretable explanation generation. We develop an automated data generation pipeline and construct HalluScope-30K, a large-scale diagnostic dataset covering eight sources and five task categories. Based on this dataset, we design a multi-granular joint reward function and train two diagnosis models, HalluScope-4B and HalluScope-8B, which achieve state-of-the-art performance on both the MHALO benchmark and our fine-grained hallucination classification benchmark. Notably, detection and classification are mutually beneficial under joint optimization. Furthermore, diagnosis-driven feedback experiments show that the fine-grained diagnostic explanations produced by our model effectively guide target models to correct their hallucinations, with full diagnosis substantially outperforming all baselines on both Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct and LLaVA-1.5-7B.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文回顾了第二届LoViF挑战赛——真实世界全能图像恢复任务，该挑战旨在推动统一框架下多种退化（模糊、低光、雾、雨、雪）的图像恢复，吸引了158名注册参与者，20支队伍进入最终排名，报告分析了提交方案和结果，总结了有效设计策略并建立了更新基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前真实世界图像恢复面临多种退化类型（模糊、低光、雾、雨、雪），现有方法多针对单一退化设计，缺乏统一框架下的泛化能力和鲁棒性评估基准。LoViF挑战赛旨在填补这一空白，推动全能型图像恢复模型的发展。

Method: 举办第二届LoViF挑战赛，构建包含多种真实退化类型的通用基准数据集，邀请参与者提交统一框架下的图像恢复方案，对所有提交结果进行复现和验证，并对成功复现的方案进行综合分析与对比。

Result: 158名注册参与者中，20支队伍的结果成功复现并进入最终排名。报告对各类方案进行了深入分析，揭示了有效的设计策略，并建立了更新后的基准。

Conclusion: 本文总结了真实世界全能图像恢复的最新进展，提炼出有效的方法设计策略，为未来低层视觉领域的相关研究提供了更新后的基准和参考。

Abstract: This paper presents a review of the second LoViF Challenge on Real-World All-in-One Image Restoration. The challenge aims to advance unified image restoration under diverse real-world degradation conditions, including blur, low-light, haze, rain, and snow. It provides a common benchmark for evaluating the restoration accuracy, robustness, and generalization capability of models across multiple degradation categories within a unified framework. The competition attracted 158 registered participants, and 20 teams were included in the final ranking after their submitted results were successfully reproduced and verified. This report provides a comprehensive analysis of the submitted solutions and corresponding results, highlighting recent advances in real-world all-in-one image restoration. The summarized methods and empirical findings reveal effective design strategies and establish an updated benchmark for future research in real-world low-level vision.

</details>


### [52] [Causal-AgentIR: Self-Evolving Causal Memory for Adaptive Image Restoration Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21125)
*Hu Gao,Yulong Chen,Lizhuang Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Causal-AgentIR，一个具有自进化因果记忆的分层多智能体框架，将图像修复经验组织为结构化因果记忆图，支持多跳因果推理和知识动态更新，实现集体图像修复智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于智能体的图像修复方法将知识存储为静态工具描述、手动定义的退化先验或非结构化的文本摘要，限制了修复知识在长期经验中的积累、验证、修正和遗忘能力。

Method: 将退化模式、图像区域、修复工具、操作、质量变化和用户偏好组织为结构化因果记忆图，支持基于图的检索和多跳因果推理；设计分层多智能体协作系统，包含规划、退化分析、工具专长、因果记忆推理、结果评判和记忆策展等智能体；根据质量变化和反馈动态增删改查记忆知识。

Result: 大量实验表明所提框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 结构化因果记忆和图检索推理能够有效提升智能体在多样化图像修复任务中的知识积累、传递和自适应能力。

Abstract: Image restoration agents have recently emerged as a flexible paradigm for handling diverse and unpredictable degradations in real-world scenarios. Existing agents typically formulate restoration as a tool-using process, where the agent perceives degradations, searches candidate tools, executes restoration operations, and revises the plan through reflection or rollback. However, their knowledge is often stored as static tool descriptions, manually defined degradation priors, or unstructured textual summaries, which limits the accumulation, verification, revision, and forgetting of restoration knowledge over long-term experience. In this paper, we propose Causal-AgentIR, a hierarchical multi-agent framework with self-evolving causal memory for collective image restoration intelligence. Instead of representing restoration experience as isolated textual records, Causal-AgentIR organizes degradation patterns, image regions, restoration tools, actions, quality changes, and user preferences into a structured causal memory graph. This graph supports graph-based retrieval and multi-hop causal reasoning, enabling agents to infer how specific restoration operations or tool sequences affect restoration quality under different degradation conditions. The framework further organizes multiple agents into a collaborative system, including planning, degradation analysis, tool expertise, causal memory reasoning, outcome critique, and memory curation. Through this design, restoration experience can be added, updated, merged, reinforced, ignored, or discarded according to observed quality changes and feedback, allowing the agent to maintain reliable and transferable restoration knowledge.
  Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [53] [Safety-oriented sidewalk and road segmentation for smartphone-based assistive navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21137)
*Hakan Calim,Anamaria Dumitrescu,Adarsh Bhandary Panambur,Huzaifa Asif,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向盲人导航的安全导向语义分割框架，构建了SENSATION-DS数据集（2752对图像-掩码，9类标签），评估五种分割架构。UPerNet-MobileNetV3取得最高mIoU（0.715），DeepLabV3Plus-MobileNetV3取得最低道路误判率（0.079）及最高Android运行速度（7.383 FPS）。研究表明辅助性 sidewalk 感知需同时评估分割精度、假安全错误和移动端部署可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲人和视障行人（BVIPs）独立出行依赖 sidewalk 移动能力，但基于智能手机的辅助导航需要能够区分可通行 sidewalk 与相邻不安全区域的感知模型，现有方法对此缺乏专门的安全导向评估。

Method: 构建SENSATION-DS数据集（2,752对图像-掩码，9类导航标签）；统一外部城市和 sidewalk 数据集标签空间；采用分阶段目标域自适应，结合掩码条件合成图像与SAM2伪标签；评估五种分割架构；使用mIoU、道路/sidewalk特定指标、Road-as-Sidewalk错误率（假安全代理指标）及Android ONNX基准测试。

Result: 合成增强提高分割精度；SAM2伪标签更稳定降低Road-as-Sidewalk错误；UPerNet-MobileNetV3离线mIoU最高（0.715±0.006）；DeepLabV3Plus-MobileNetV3取得最低Road-as-Sidewalk错误率（0.079）及最高Android运行速度（7.383 FPS, 512×384）。

Conclusion: 辅助性 sidewalk 感知应联合评估分割精度、假安全行为代理指标和智能手机部署可行性；模型选择需平衡准确感知、保守错误行为和实际运行速度；真实世界效益仍需BVIP用户验证。

Abstract: Independent sidewalk mobility is essential for blind and visually impaired pedestrians (BVIPs), yet smartphone-based assistive navigation requires perception models that distinguish walkable sidewalks from adjacent unsafe regions. This study presents a safety-oriented semantic segmentation framework for future mobile guidance. We introduce SENSATION-DS, a chest-height pedestrian-view dataset with 2,752 image-mask pairs and nine-class navigation-relevant taxonomy. External urban and sidewalk datasets were harmonized to this label space, and five segmentation architectures were evaluated using staged target-domain adaptation with mask-conditioned synthetic images and Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM2) pseudo-labels. Models were assessed using mean Intersection over Union (mIoU), road- and sidewalk-specific metrics, Road-as-Sidewalk Error Rate as a proxy false-safe measure, and Android Open Neural Network Exchange benchmarking. Synthetic augmentation generally improved segmentation accuracy, whereas SAM2 pseudo-labels more consistently reduced Road-as-Sidewalk errors. UPerNet-MobileNetV3 achieved the highest offline mIoU (0.715 +/- 0.006), while DeepLabV3Plus-MobileNetV3 achieved the lowest Road-as-Sidewalk Error Rate (0.079) and highest Android runtime at 512x384 (7.383 FPS). These results show that assistive sidewalk perception should be evaluated jointly by segmentation accuracy, proxy false-safe behavior, and smartphone deployment feasibility, while real-world benefit requires validation with BVIP users. This evaluation supports selecting models that balance accurate perception, conservative error behavior, and practical runtime.

</details>


### [54] [DTIF: Robust Loop Closure Detection via Delaunay Triangle Topology in Complex Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21138)
*Xin Zhao,Jianping Li,Qin Zou,Fuxun Liang,Zhen Dong,Bisheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DTIF框架，利用树干三角剖分拓扑结构，解决GNSS缺失环境下低成本LiDAR点云的闭环检测与全局配准问题，在资源受限边缘平台上实现高效鲁棒的重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本边缘平台在林下GNSS缺失环境中独立构建局部地图后，需要无初始化闭环检测与全局配准，但低质量点云稀疏含噪、树干布局重复导致感知混淆和错误匹配，现有方法难以兼顾鲁棒性、效率和边缘部署需求。

Method: (1) 提取树干作为稳定地标，利用Delaunay三角剖分编码拓扑关系实现紧凑场景表示；(2) 基于边长和半径统计筛选候选子图，通过边-半径一致性验证和强/弱顶点支持聚合构建加权顶点对应；(3) 引入拓扑导出的可靠性权重，在重力对齐下分别估计偏航角、水平平移和高度平移。

Result: 在模拟和真实森林数据集上，DTIF实现了精确配准且计算开销低，在鲁棒性、效率和边缘平台可部署性之间取得了良好平衡。

Conclusion: DTIF是一种轻量级的基于树干拓扑的森林闭环检测与全局配准框架，适合资源受限的边缘平台部署。

Abstract: Accurate forest inventory and large-scale mapping are essential for ecosystem monitoring and sustainable forest management. Multiple low-cost edge platforms enable efficient large-area data acquisition, but merging independently constructed local maps in GNSS-denied understory environments still requires initialization-free loop closure detection and global registration. This task is challenging because low-cost LiDAR point clouds are sparse and noisy, while repetitive trunk layouts and the lack of distinctive geometric landmarks lead to severe perceptual aliasing and false correspondences. To address these issues, we propose DTIF (Delaunay Triangulation in Forests), a lightweight trunk-topology-based framework for forest loop closure detection and global registration. Tree trunks are first extracted as stable landmarks and encoded using a Delaunay topology for compact scene representation. Candidate submaps are then screened using edge-length and radius statistics, followed by edge--radius consistency verification and strong/weak vertex support aggregation to construct weighted vertex correspondences. Finally, topology-derived reliability weights are incorporated into a decoupled robust pose estimator that separately estimates yaw, horizontal translation, and elevation translation under gravity alignment. Experiments on simulated and real-world forest datasets demonstrate that DTIF achieves accurate registration with low computational overhead, providing a favorable balance among robustness, efficiency, and deployability on resource-constrained edge platforms.

</details>


### [55] [CRAG-MM-Diagnostics: Enabling Stage-Wise Analysis of Knowledge-Intensive VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21155)
*Hanseok Oh,Parishad BehnamGhader,Benno Krojer,Hyunji Lee,Paul Liang,Siva Reddy,Verna Dankers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CRAG-MM-Diagnostics诊断基准，通过阶段式标注隔离KI-VQA的三个子问题（视觉定位、目标识别、知识检索与推理），发现知识检索与推理是主要瓶颈，同时视觉定位和目标识别也存在问题，并据此提出结合视觉定位模块的接地双模态RAG流水线，显著提升GPT-5和Qwen的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KI-VQA基准仅报告端到端准确率，无法揭示模型在视觉定位、目标识别、知识检索与推理等子问题中的具体失败原因，缺乏细粒度的诊断能力。

Method: 构建CRAG-MM-Diagnostics诊断基准，包含阶段式数据标注，分别隔离三个子问题：1）语言引导的视觉定位，2）目标识别，3）知识检索与推理。收集目标ROI、实体名称、视觉复杂度等元数据，对全参数和检索增强型VLM进行细粒度分析。

Result: 知识检索与推理是KI-VQA的主要瓶颈；VLM在目标识别上存在困难；图像检索器难以整合文本线索。基于发现提出的接地双模态RAG流水线（集成视觉定位模块裁剪目标后进行图像检索）使GPT-5和Qwen的准确率分别提升13.3和8.5个百分点。

Conclusion: KI-VQA系统存在多阶段瓶颈，需采用阶段感知评估方法。结合视觉定位的接地检索增强生成可有效缓解问题，提升VLM在多模态知识问答中的表现。

Abstract: Knowledge-Intensive Visual Question Answering (KI-VQA) benchmarks evaluate Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as multimodal knowledge assistants by requiring external information beyond a provided image to answer questions. KI-VQA involves multiple sub-problems -referring expression understanding, visual grounding, object recognition, knowledge retrieval, and reasoning-yet existing benchmarks typically report only end-task accuracy, obscuring where failures arise. To analyze the full KI-VQA pipeline, we introduce CRAG-MM-Diagnostics, a diagnostic benchmark with stage-wise data annotations that isolate 1) language-based visual grounding, 2) object identification, and 3) knowledge retrieval and reasoning. We evaluate fully parametric and retrieval-augmented VLMs, providing fine-grained analyses using newly collected metadata, such as target ROIs, entity names, and visual complexity scores. Our results point to knowledge retrieval and reasoning as the primary bottleneck, but also highlight issues in the other parts of the KI-VQA pipeline, such as the fact that VLMs struggle with target object identification or that image retrievers struggle to integrate textual cues. These findings expose fundamental limitations in current KI-VQA systems and motivate stage-aware evaluation.
