However, evaluating this heterogeneous data remains a significant challenge. Existing caption metrics focus on flat textual outputs and fail to reliably assess multimodal attributes. To bridge this gap, we propose a multi-axis evaluation framework tailored for structured audio descriptions. Building on the AudioCards dataset, we evaluate outputs across five orthogonal axes: tag-sets, descriptions, logical reasoning, numeric measurements, and spectral profiles. Our approach combines Large Language Model (LLM) judges to capture semantic nuance with deterministic computational metrics to precisely measure acoustic deviations. To rigorously validate the reliability of this framework, we introduce a controlled perturbation testing protocol that injects typed, graded errors into groundtruth annotations. Our results demonstrate that this framework successfully distinguishes meaning-preserving paraphrases from genuine semantic and acoustic corruptions.

</details>


### [164] [Token Budget Saturation and Mechanistic Early Detection of Reasoning Non-Convergence in Chain-of-Thought Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21433)
*Renuka Oladri,Niveda Jawahar,Abdirisak Mohamed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B的思维链推理存在双峰收敛模式：收敛的生成在AIME上达到90.3%准确率，非收敛的仅6.6%，总体收敛率62.0%。通过线性探针分析模型隐藏状态，发现第20层第150个token的激活能提前检测收敛结果（AUC 0.608），且优于基于熵和重复统计的行为基线，但显著性检验未达常规阈值（p=0.063），表明收敛命运在中间表征中部分可预测，有望实现早期退出推理和自适应计算分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究思维链推理模型生成是否收敛的早期可检测性，旨在前瞻性地识别非收敛生成，为早期退出推理和自适应计算分配铺路。

Method: 1) 实证刻画DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B的双峰收敛模式；2) 在token位置50-300处训练线性探针，利用隐藏状态激活预测收敛结果；3) 对比基于token熵和重复统计的行为基线；4) 进行扫掠级置换检验评估显著性。

Result: 收敛生成准确率90.3%，非收敛仅6.6%，收敛率62.0%。层20在token 150处的线性探针AUC达0.608（±0.080），即使在token 50也高于随机；激活探针一致优于行为基线；置换检验p=0.063。

Conclusion: 收敛命运在生成结束前已部分编码于中间表征中，但信号较弱，在现有样本量下未达常规统计显著阈值；该发现为早期退出推理和自适应计算分配提供了可行性依据。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought reasoning models such as DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B exhibit a bimodal convergence pattern: generations either terminate within a token budget (converged) or exhaust it without reaching a conclusion (non-converged). We characterize this phenomenon empirically, showing that converged generations achieve 90.3% accuracy on AIME 1983-2024 while non-converged ones achieve only 6.6%, with an overall convergence rate of 62.0%. We then ask whether this outcome is detectable early in the thinking chain using internal model representations. Training linear probes on hidden-state activations at token positions 50-300, we find that layer-20 activations at token 150 achieve AUC 0.608 (+-0.080, 5-fold CV), reliably above chance even at token 50. Activation probes consistently outperform behavioral baselines derived from token entropy and repetition statistics. A sweep-level permutation test yields p=0.063 (100,000 permutations), consistent with a modest signal that our sample size cannot confirm at conventional thresholds. These findings suggest that convergence fate is partially encoded in intermediate representations well before the generation ends, opening a path toward early-exit inference and adaptive compute allocation.

</details>


### [165] [When Trivia Is Not Trivial: Everyday Knowledge Failures in Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21445)
*Anna Mosolova,Djamé Seddah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出多语言基准TriviaRoomQA，评估30个开源LLM在288个主题上的常识与长尾知识表现，发现模型在历史、地理等知识密集型主题表现强，但在流行文化主题（名人、音乐、电影等）表现弱，且知识获取存在语言依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学术基准已趋饱和，无法全面评估LLM在真实场景中对常识性、文化性及长尾知识的掌握程度，尤其在多语言和流行文化领域的知识缺口未被充分研究。

Method: 构建TriviaRoomQA多语言基准，包含3,300道平行多选题（6种欧洲语言）及5,340道法语专属题，覆盖288个主题；评估30个开源LLM（7B-70B参数），按知识密集型和流行文化型主题分类分析，并跨语言对比同一问题的表现。

Result: 模型在历史、地理、数学等知识密集型主题表现优异，但在名人、音乐、电影、新闻等流行文化主题上显著较弱；同一问题在不同语言下的表现存在差异，说明事实知识的获取并非完全语言无关。

Conclusion: 现有学术基准未能捕捉LLM在流行文化和跨语言常识知识上的重要缺口，TriviaRoomQA可作为更全面的评估工具，揭示模型知识分布的不均衡性。

Abstract: Quiz rooms, trivia nights, and quiz shows challenge human knowledge across a wide range of topics, from canonical facts to everyday culture. In this paper, we examine whether large language models (LLMs) can perform competitively in such settings, using quiz-style questions to test them on both common and niche topics. We introduce TriviaRoomQA, a multilingual benchmark designed to evaluate everyday, culturally grounded, and long-tail knowledge across 288 topics. The benchmark contains 3,300 parallel multiple-choice questions in six European languages and additional 5,340 French-only questions for a more fine-grained case study. We evaluate 30 open-weight LLMs from European, Asian, and North American providers, covering models from 7 to 70B parameters. We find that models are strong on knowledge-intensive topics such as history, geography, and mathematics, but substantially weaker on everyday popular-culture topics such as celebrities, music, movies, and news. Moreover, model performance varies across languages even for the same underlying questions, suggesting that access to factual knowledge is not always language-independent. In sum, our dataset and experiments demonstrate an important knowledge gap which is not captured by existing academic-based saturated benchmarks.

</details>


### [166] [RUMBA: Russian User Memory Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21447)
*Elizaveta Shevtsova,Inna Glebkina,Mark Baushenko,Pavel Gulyaev,Alena Fenogenova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RUMBA是一个面向俄语长时对话记忆的细粒度基准，包含时间戳对话和跨会话检索/推理的QA对，用于诊断不同记忆机制的优势与失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时记忆基准以英语为中心且依赖聚合检索指标，无法捕捉长程上下文、时间信息和推理之间的交互。

Method: 构建了带时间戳的用户-助手对话数据集，设计了细粒度的记忆问题类型分类法，统一考虑了语义类型、会话范围、时间推理和时间表达显式性，并提供了对齐的英语子集。

Result: 评估了当代记忆系统和长上下文模型，展示了RUMBA作为诊断工具分析模型在不同切片上的行为，识别不同记忆机制的优势与失败模式。

Conclusion: RUMBA为长时对话记忆评估提供了更全面的方法论，弥补了当前基准在跨会话时间推理方面的不足。

Abstract: The ability to handle long-term memory in LLMs is becoming increasingly critical, yet existing benchmarks remain English-centric and rely on aggregate retrieval metrics, failing to capture interactions between long-range context, temporal information, and reasoning. To address this, we introduce RUMBA (Russian User Memory BenchmArk) - a new benchmark for long-term conversational memory that provides a fine-grained taxonomy of memory-centric question types and a unified methodology accounting for semantic type, session scope, temporal reasoning, and the explicitness of temporal expressions. RUMBA consists of timestamped user-assistant dialogues with QA pairs requiring retrieval, combination, and reasoning across sessions. While designed for Russian, we also provide an aligned English subset under the same methodology. We evaluate contemporary memory systems and long-context models, and show how RUMBA serves as a diagnostic tool to analyze model behavior across benchmark slices and identify strengths and failure modes of different memory mechanisms.

</details>


### [167] [What, Where, and How: Disentangling the Roles of Task, Language, and Model in Code Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21491)
*Piotr Wilam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过2×2实验设计（Python/Rust语言 × Qwen2.5-Coder/DeepSeek-Coder模型），系统研究了代码语言模型中概念电路（concept circuits）的普适性问题。研究发现：任务决定哪些概念拥有专用电路（Yes），模型决定电路的位置和增长模式（No），即普适性取决于表征内容而非计算组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究独立训练的语言模型是否以相同方式表征相同事物，具体从"什么（What）、哪里（Where）、如何（How）"三个维度分离任务、语言和模型对概念电路的影响，回答"电路是否普适"这一根本问题。

Method: 采用2×2交叉设计（Python/Rust × Qwen2.5-Coder-7B/DeepSeek-Coder-V1-6.7B），提取并测量58个Python和57个Rust语法概念在四个模型-语言组合中的完整电路清单，结合消融实验和线性探针验证电路功能性。

Result: (1) What：模型在哪些概念拥有电路上高度一致（Python ρ=0.638, Rust ρ=0.673, p<10⁻⁷）；(2) Where：Qwen集中在L17-19层，DeepSeek在L6-7层；(3) How：Qwen的原子概念有早期尖峰，DeepSeek没有。Rust概念电路量是Python的2-3倍。DeepSeek在语言间共享神经元程度是Qwen的1.94倍。Qwen将Rust的9个类型/特质关键词绑定为一个紧密神经元簇。

Conclusion: 电路普适性问题无单一答案：What维度是普适的（由任务决定），Where和How维度不是（由模型决定）。普适性是表征内容的属性，而非计算组织的属性。

Abstract: Do independently trained language models come to represent the same thing in the same way? We answer for code, extending a recently introduced concept-circuit extraction method to a 2x2 design -- Python and Rust crossed with Qwen2.5-Coder-7B and DeepSeek-Coder-V1-6.7B -- and measuring a complete inventory of grammatical concepts (58 Python, 57 Rust) identically in all four cells: the smallest design that separates what depends on the task, the language, and the model.
  The answer splits into three parts. What earns dedicated circuitry is set by the task: the models agree on which concepts receive circuits (Spearman $ρ$ = 0.638 for Python, 0.673 for Rust, both p < $10^{-7}$). Where those circuits sit is set by the model: Qwen processes concepts in a late band (~L17-19), DeepSeek at L6-7, for both languages. How circuits grow across layers is also set by the model: Qwen gives its atomic concepts an early spike that DeepSeek does not. "Are circuits universal?" thus has no single answer: yes for What, no for Where and How -- universality is a property of representational content, not of computational organisation.
  None of this structure was fixed in advance. The agreement could have landed anywhere between independence and identity; it lands at $ρ\approx 0.65$. Rust constructs receive 2-3x more concept-specific circuitry than their Python equivalents, in both models. Both models share neurons between the languages (6/7 and 7/7 paired constructs), DeepSeek 1.94x more than Qwen -- a direction no prior result predicts. And Qwen binds nine keywords of Rust's type-and-trait machinery into one tight neuron cluster (Jaccard 0.535 vs null 0.112, p < 0.001), a semantic dimension invisible in surface syntax. Ablation and linear probes confirm the circuits are functional.
  All claims are scoped to this 2x2; whether the per-model profile predicts a third model is the designed next test.

</details>


### [168] [Artificial Epanorthosis: Why large language models overuse a classical rhetorical figure, and how to mitigate it](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21498)
*Federico Boggia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型中系统性地过度使用"epanorthosis"（自我修正修辞格），主要源于训练数据中宣传性文本和RLHF偏好调优。通过构建Epanorthosis Index（密度/人类基线），测量发现模型在演说体裁中过度使用（意大利语版近三倍），在非正式问答中不足，而在论证、新闻和百科中匹配人类。作者提出轻量级LoRA适配器缓解、意大利语单指令减少50-75%、SFT几乎完全消除等方法，目标不是消除而是按体裁校准到人类水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示LLM中一种古老修辞格（epanorthosis）的系统性过度使用现象，分析其成因（训练数据分布、RLHF偏好调优、从左到右生成机制），并指出真正的风险是人类开始像机器一样写作。

Method: 提出Epanorthosis Index（密度/特定体裁人类基线）进行量化测量；在三种规模的指令调优模型家族上测试；使用LoRA适配器、单指令提示、SFT适配器+缩放系数进行缓解和校准。

Result: 演说体裁中模型过度使用（英文约两倍，意大利文近三倍，集中于较大模型）；非正式问答中不足；论证、新闻和百科体裁中匹配人类。单指令减少50-75%，SFT适配器几乎完全消除，缩放系数可精确回调到人类基线。

Conclusion: 目标不是消除epanorthosis，而是按体裁校准到人类使用率。真正的风险是人类可能被机器影响而改变自身写作风格。

Abstract: A rhetorical figure that Cicero and Quintilian catalogued two thousand years ago reappears, systematically, in the text of large language models: epanorthosis, the self-correction of the specimen «This is not a course. It is a journey of transformation». This essay argues that the overuse is a trained disposition, driven mainly by a training distribution rich in promotional prose and by preference tuning (RLHF) that rewards confident, emphatic phrasing; the left-to-right nature of generation is an amplifier rather than the root cause. Building on evidence that models diverge from human rhetorical style, and on Fontanier's classification of epanorthosis as a figure of thought, it sets out a programme that scores the figure against genre-specific human baselines through an Epanorthosis Index (density relative to the human rate). A first measurement, on three sizes of one instruction-tuned model family, finds mis-calibration by register in both directions: the models overshoot in oratory (about twofold, near threefold in Italian, concentrated in the larger tiers) and undershoot in informal question-and-answer writing, while matching humans in argument, journalism, and encyclopedic prose. Three constructive contributions follow: a survey of mitigation techniques centred on lightweight LoRA adapters; a demonstration, in Italian, that a one-line instruction cuts the figure by half to nearly three-quarters and that a supervised-fine-tuning adapter removes it almost entirely, with a scaling coefficient that dials the reduction back onto the human rate; and the argument that the target is calibration to the human rate for each genre, not elimination. It closes on the stakes: the real risk is that we begin to write like the machines.

</details>


### [169] [DONDO: Open w2v-BERT Speech-Recognition Base Models for African Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21540)
*Paul Azunre*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DONDO是一个面向非洲语言的开放ASR基座模型家族，基于w2v-BERT 2.0自监督语音编码器，包含21个单语模型和5个多语模型，覆盖27种语言变体。通过两步/三步学习率退火微调策略，多语模型在多个语言族上达到10-13%的WER，接近单语基线水平。所有模型在Apache-2.0许可下发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲语言缺乏标注语音数据，现有ASR系统覆盖不足。作者利用宗教文本朗读语音（许可清晰、正字法一致）作为训练数据，旨在开发开放、可商用的非洲语言ASR基座模型。

Method: 基于w2v-BERT 2.0自监督语音编码器，采用两步（或三步）学习率退火微调策略：先以高学习率适应共享多语模型，再退火恢复（甚至超越）强单语基线。同时提出轻量级语言条件机制，将one-hot语言标识作为前缀帧注入声学特征，实现单一多语检查点在推理时定向到目标语言。

Result: 五个多语模型族经退火微调后平均WER达10-13%，几乎弥合了与单语模型之间的差距，且单个检查点覆盖多种语言。覆盖语言的第一语言使用者约1亿人，含第二语言使用者则更多。

Conclusion: DONDO展示了利用宗教文本朗读语音进行低资源语言ASR训练的可行性，学习率退火和轻量级语言条件机制有效提升了多语模型的性能。所有模型开源（Apache-2.0），便于社区进一步微调和商用。

Abstract: We present DONDO, a family of open, permissively licensed automatic speech recognition (ASR) base models for African languages, built on the w2v-BERT 2.0 self-supervised speech encoder. DONDO comprises twenty-one monolingual models and five multilingual models spanning twenty-seven language varieties across Ghana, Sierra Leone, Nigeria, Senegal, Kenya and Zimbabwe. Models are fine-tuned primarily on read speech drawn from religious texts, which offer broad, license-clear and orthographically consistent coverage for languages that otherwise lack transcribed audio. We describe a two-step (and, for one family, three-step) learning-rate-annealed fine-tuning procedure that first adapts a shared multilingual model at a high learning rate and then anneals it to recover, and in several cases surpass, strong monolingual baselines. We further describe a lightweight language-conditioning mechanism that injects a one-hot language identity as a sequence of prefix frames prepended to the acoustic features, allowing a single multilingual checkpoint to be steered to a target language at inference. Across the five multilingual families the annealed models reach average word error rates (WER) of 10-13%, closing most of the gap to monolingual models while covering many languages in a single checkpoint. All models are released on the Hugging Face KhayaAI organisation under the Apache-2.0 license (attribution only) so that others may fine-tune them freely, including for commercial use. We provide a conservative estimate that the languages covered are spoken by on the order of one hundred million first-language speakers, and by substantially more when second-language use is included.

</details>


### [170] [MedGame: Storytelling Gamification Empowered by Large Language Models for Medical Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21570)
*Qian Wu,Xinrong Zhou,Zizhan Ma,Kai Chen,Zheyao Gao,Xun Lin,Hongqiu Wu,Longfei Gou,Yixiao Liu,Ann Sin Nga Lau,Qi Dou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MedGame是一个将静态临床病例转化为结构化、可执行叙事游戏的框架，采用双引擎设计（医学叙事设计器+故事导演），构建了5000例基准测试集，实验表明微调可提升开源LLM性能，学生感知其比纯文本方案更吸引人且更有用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM医学教育系统大多局限于问答或单轮反馈等局部交互，缺乏将整个临床病例组织成以决策为中心的学习轨迹的整体框架。

Method: 提出MedGame框架，包含双引擎：医学叙事设计器（Medical Narrative Designer）合成基于病例的临床故事情节（含状态和决策节点），故事导演（Story Director）将其转化为依赖感知的多模态编排计划，由交互平台渲染执行。同时构建MedGame Bench（5000例病例基准）用于评估。

Result: 任务特定微调显著提升开源LLM在MedGame Bench上的表现，缩小了与商业模型的差距。学生试点研究显示学习者认为MedGame比纯文本方案更吸引人且更有用。

Conclusion: MedGame框架能有效将临床病例转化为决策导向的叙事游戏，提升医学教育中的学习体验和参与度。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for medical education, but most existing systems focus on localized interactions such as question answering or single-turn feedback, rather than organizing an entire clinical case into a decision-centered learning trajectory. We introduce \textit{MedGame}, a framework that transforms static clinical cases into structured, executable storytelling games. MedGame uses a dual-engine design: a Medical Narrative Designer synthesizes case-grounded clinical storylines with states and decision nodes, while a Story Director converts them into dependency-aware multimodal orchestration plans rendered by our released interactive platform. We construct MedGame Bench, a 5,000-case benchmark and evaluation protocol for Medical Narrative Generation and Story Direction. Experiments show that task-specific fine-tuning substantially improves open-source LLMs on MedGame Bench and narrows the gap with commercial models. A pilot student study further shows that learners perceive MedGame as more engaging and useful than text-only alternatives.

</details>


### [171] [Surprisal Theory is Tautological (without Rational Grounding)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21574)
*Ryan Cotterell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文论证了惊奇理论（Surprisal theory）在无额外约束条件下是一个同义反复（tautology）：对于任何非负的加工难度度量，总存在某个语言模型使其惊奇度与之成仿射关系。由于任何难度模式都能与某个语言模型一致，该理论无法做出可证伪的预测。打破这种同义反复需要理性主义干预，即语言模型必须来自非经验驱动的理解者模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示惊奇理论的内在逻辑缺陷：该理论声称语言单位在上下文中的加工难度是其惊奇度的仿射函数，但作者证明这一说法在没有额外约束时是同义反复，因为任何难度模式都可以通过构造合适的语言模型来拟合，从而使理论失去可证伪性。

Method: 通过形式化论证表明：对于任意上下文中的非负难度度量，在温和技术条件下总存在一个语言模型，其惊奇度是该难度度量的仿射函数。随后指出过去二十年心理语言学隐含假设——相关语言模型是生成训练语料的分布，即改进语料拟合能改进行为预测——已被近期实证研究推翻。

Result: 证明了惊奇理论在无额外约束条件下是同义反复；指出更好的语料模型可能是更差的行为预测器这一实证发现；提出打破同义反复需要理性主义干预，即语言模型应源自非经验驱动的理解者模型（如基于记忆约束或加工目标）。

Conclusion: 惊奇理论若要成为有意义的可证伪理论，必须引入理性主义约束：相关的语言模型不能仅由语料统计决定，而应从对理解者认知机制的独立理论推导得出，从而避免循环论证。

Abstract: Surprisal theory holds that the human processing difficulty of a linguistic unit in context is an affine function of its surprisal under some language model. I argue this claim is a tautology without further constraint: for any non-negative difficulty measure over units in context, there exists a language model whose surprisal is an affine function of it under mild technical conditions. Therefore, because any pattern of difficulty is consistent with some language model, without an additional constraint on the language model, surprisal theory makes no falsifiable predictions. The tautology was long obscured by an assumption implicit in two decades of psycholinguistic work---that the relevant language model is the distribution that generated the training corpus, so that improving corpus fit improves predictions of human behavior. Recent empirical work has undermined this assumption, demonstrating that better corpus models can be worse predictors of processing difficulty. I conclude that breaking the tautology requires a rationalist intervention, i.e., the relevant language model must be derived from a non-empirically motivated model of the comprehender, which could be based on, for instance, memory constraints or processing goals, and that, thus, does not depend on the behavioral data surprisal theory is meant to explain.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Attribution Markets: A Fisher-Market Formulation for Fractional Credit Assignment Between Planned Tasks and Performed Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20694)
*Salavat Ishbulatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将计划与执行记录之间的桥梁建模为准线性Fisher市场，其中计划任务作为预算受限的买家，已执行动作作为可分割商品，通过融合文本/结构/时间信号设定估价，并引入熵正则化以增强噪声鲁棒性，最终统一了市场均衡与最优传输方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计划与执行记录系统之间存在断裂：计划的任务预算与实际记录的动作时长和描述缺乏精确关联。现有方法采用排他性的全有或全无链接，导致真正相关的未链接记录被遗漏，以及活跃目标上产生虚假的停滞信号。

Method: 将问题建模为准线性Fisher市场：计划任务为预算约束的买家，已执行动作为可分割商品，通过融合文本/结构/时间信号定义估价。引入卖方保留价和买方现金选项两个市场机制，实现守恒性、硬预算上限和可证明的垃圾过滤器。扩展出凹完成效用折扣函数，解决标准收敛理论不适用的问题，通过满足阈值不动点（Brouwer）和局部唯一性（对角占优条件）加以验证。进一步提出单参数熵正则化推广，统一市场均衡与熵正则化最优传输，并给出噪声自适应的正则化强度规则。

Result: 理论层面证明了市场机制满足守恒性、硬预算上限和垃圾过滤器的定理；在随机和对抗性实例上验证了满足阈值不动点的存在性与局部唯一性。实验发现原始市场的尖锐零熵均衡对亲和性噪声比熵正则化最优传输更敏感，这是其固有弱点。熵正则化推广统一了两者，噪声自适应规则提升了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的Fisher市场框架为计划-执行记录桥接提供了理论基础，熵正则化推广解决了噪声敏感性弱点，统一了市场均衡与最优传输方法。报告了完整可复现参数，坦诚讨论了局限性，并将结果关联到多触点归因、最优传输和在线Fisher市场算法等领域。

Abstract: Personal and organizational planning systems maintain two records that drift apart: what was planned (a task's effort budget) and what was done (a logged action's duration and description). Existing systems bridge them with an exclusive, all-or-nothing link that strands genuinely related but unlinked effort and reports false stalls on active goals. We formulate the bridge as a quasi-linear Fisher market: planned tasks are budget-constrained buyers, performed actions are divisible goods, and a fused text/structural/temporal signal sets each buyer's valuation. Two market instruments - a seller reserve price and a buyer cash option - yield conservation, a hard budget cap, and a provable junk filter as theorems. We extend the market with a concave completion utility discounting progress as a task nears its plan; standard convergence theory for the market's algorithm does not transfer here, resolved by a satiation-threshold fixed point with existence (Brouwer) and local uniqueness under an explicit diagonal-dominance condition, validated empirically on random and adversarial instances. A de-circularized, multi-seed benchmark - observed affinity corrupted independently of the scored ground truth - surfaces a genuine weak spot: the market's sharp, zero-entropy equilibrium is more sensitive to affinity noise than entropy-regularized optimal transport's permanently smoothed one. We resolve this with a one-parameter entropy-regularized generalization unifying the two, plus a noise-adaptive rule for its regularization strength. We report full reproducibility parameters, discuss limitations candidly, and relate the result to multi-touch attribution, optimal transport, and online Fisher-market algorithms.

</details>


### [173] [DataPrep-Bench: Benchmarking LLMs as Training Data Preparators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20465)
*Hao Liang,Qifeng Cai,Yibo Lin,Jianzhuo Du,Qifeng Xia,Sizhe Qiu,Linzhuang Sun,Meiyi Qiang,Zhaoyang Han,Xiaochen Ma,Bohan Zeng,Ruichuan An,Conghui He,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DataPrep-Bench，首个统一基准，用于评估LLM驱动数据准备的两个核心能力：数据构建（将原始来源转化为监督训练数据）和数据质量评估（预测数据集的训练效用），覆盖6个领域和多个基座模型。同时提出Data-Construction-Skill智能体（提升近20个绝对百分点）和Distributional Alignment Score（DAS）评估指标（在4/6领域达到最强跨模型相关性）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据质量从根本上决定了大语言模型的能力，但目前缺乏统一的基准来端到端地衡量LLM、智能体和数据驱动流程在数据准备方面的表现。

Method: 将LLM驱动的数据准备拆分为两个互补能力：数据构建（将原始来源转化为监督训练数据）和数据质量评估（在训练前预测数据集的训练价值）。提出DataPrep-Bench统一基准，采用共享的下游任务验证协议，覆盖6个领域和多个基座模型。数据构建方面，在Dolly-15k基础上微调基座模型评分；数据质量评估方面，通过Pearson相关系数与下游性能进行评分。

Result: Data-Construction-Skill智能体在Llama-3.1-8B Finance任务上比Dolly-only基线提升近20个绝对百分点；DAS在4/6领域达到最强跨模型相关性，是唯一在数学、科学和医学三个领域同时超过r>0.70的指标，优于现有基于质量、多样性和启发式的评估方法。

Conclusion: DataPrep-Bench为衡量LLM驱动数据准备中的两个核心能力提供了统一的、以下游任务验证为基础的评估框架，将两者视为同等重要的目标。

Abstract: The quality of training data fundamentally determines the capabilities of large language models (LLMs), yet no unified benchmark exists to measure how well LLMs, agents, and data-centric workflows actually prepare training data end to end. We view LLM-driven data preparation as comprising two complementary capabilities: data construction, which transforms raw sources into supervised training data, and data quality evaluation, which predicts the training value of candidate datasets before downstream training; throughout, "quality" refers to downstream training utility rather than surface-level textual properties. We introduce DataPrep-Bench, the first unified benchmark that jointly evaluates both capabilities under a shared downstream-grounded protocol over six domains and multiple base models. For data construction, methods consume identical raw sources and are scored by fine-tuning a base model on their outputs jointly with Dolly-15k; alongside this track we release Data-Construction-Skill, a skill-guided agent that lifts the Dolly-only baseline by nearly 20 points absolute on Llama-3.1-8B Finance and is competitive with the strongest agent- and DataFlow-based methods in knowledge-extraction-dense domains. For data quality evaluation, scoring functions are scored by Pearson correlation with downstream performance on a shared candidate pool; we release the Distributional Alignment Score (DAS), a distribution-based evaluator that uses MMD between a candidate dataset and a domain proxy. DAS attains the strongest cross-model correlation in four of six domains and is the only metric clearing r > 0.70 simultaneously in Math, Science, and Medical, outperforming existing quality-, diversity-, and heuristic-based evaluators. DataPrep-Bench provides a unified, downstream-grounded framework for measuring progress on both capabilities as co-equal targets of LLM-driven data preparation.

</details>


### [174] [PhantomFill: When the Form Demands an Answer, Language Models Invent One](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20492)
*Rana Muhammad Usman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了一个关键发现：大语言模型在结构化输出（JSON、函数参数等）时，被迫编造信息的频率远高于自由文本。即使模型在自由回答中诚实表示"无数据"，一旦改为必填字段格式，就会100%编造答案。这种现象在多个模型中普遍存在，且规模更大的模型并不一定更诚实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的语言模型不写散文，而是填充JSON字段、函数参数和提取模板。作者发现格式本身会导致幻觉，但这一现象尚未被系统测量和研究。

Method: 设计无法回答的问题（如无可见回复的帖子、未转录的通话记录），让13个模型回答相同问题但改变答案格式（自由文本 vs 必填JSON字段），测量编造率。同时测试"证据不足"选项和直接指令的效果。

Result: GPT-5.5在自由文本中98%诚实回答；但给定必填JSON字段后，40/40次编造。10/13个模型在必填字段下编造率达100%。9个开源模型忽略"证据不足"选项。直接指令"不要推断情感"被4/6个模型的schema覆盖。编造行为与模型规模呈非线性关系。

Conclusion: 格式压力下的诚实度是一个无人测量的训练结果。编造恰好发生在无法回避的地方（必填枚举、最小数量数组）。作者发布了PhantomFill基准测试，包含两个可报告指标：强制编造率和逃避利用率。测试表明只需一行schema就能修复，但失败现象无处不在。

Abstract: Language models in production do not write prose. They fill forms: JSON fields, function arguments, extraction templates. We show that the form itself causes hallucination.
  We ask thirteen models the same question about the same input and change only the answer format. The inputs are built so the question cannot be answered: a viral post showing 12,400 likes but no visible replies, a support ticket whose call was never transcribed. In free text, GPT-5.5 answers honestly. It says there is no reply data, 98% of the time. Given a required JSON field for sentiment, the same model invents an answer 40 times out of 40. It fabricates the mood of crowds it never saw and quotes customers it never heard.
  The pattern holds with force. Required fields drive fabrication to 100% in ten of thirteen models. An explicit "insufficient evidence" option rescues only the frontier: all nine open-weight models ignore it. A direct instruction, do not infer sentiment, is overridden by the schema in four of six models. Resistance does not come with scale: within a single model family, the smallest model refuses, the mid-sized model fabricates, the largest refuses again. Honesty under format pressure is a training outcome that no one is measuring.
  The fabrication hides exactly where hedging is impossible: in required enums and minimum-count arrays, fields where no disclaimer fits. We release PhantomFill, a benchmark with deterministic scoring and two reportable numbers: the Coerced Fabrication Rate and the Escape Utilization Rate. The fix we test is one line of schema. The failure we measure is everywhere.

</details>


### [175] [The Active Ingredient in Muon's Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20512)
*Yufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过消融实验和机制分析，发现Muon优化器在模算术任务上比AdamW更快达到"顿悟"阈值的关键在于正交化（Newton-Schulz迭代），而非谱范数约束；正交化优化器通过收敛到更低谱范数的解实现泛化；减少Newton-Schulz迭代次数虽能加速但会导致解脆弱；谱缩放可被移除而不影响性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作将Muon优化器的加速归因于"谱范数约束加正交化动量"，但未分离出哪个机制真正起作用。本文旨在通过系统消融和压力测试，解耦并识别Muon加速的关键因素。

Method: 采用多种子、多学习率的扫描实验；进行消融实验（正交化vs谱范数单独使用）；机制分析（测量谱范数、嵌入变化量）；减少Newton-Schulz迭代次数（从5降到1）测试稳定性；提出稳定性感知的"持续顿悟"度量指标。

Result: 1) 仅保留正交化即可复现完整Muon性能，仅保留谱范数约束不比AdamW快且不可靠；2) 正交化优化器在约3倍更低的谱范数下达到泛化；3) 减少Newton-Schulz迭代至1次加速但导致瞬态坍塌，仅在小学习率下稳定；5次迭代是学习率鲁棒的选择；4) 谱缩放可移除且不影响性能。

Conclusion: Muon加速"顿悟"的核心机制是正交化（Newton-Schulz迭代）而非谱范数约束；正交化帮助优化器找到更低谱范数的解；迭代次数选择需在速度与稳定性间权衡；报告"首次跨越"和"持续顿悟"两种指标可避免误导性结论。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer reaches the grokking threshold on modular arithmetic faster than AdamW. Prior work attributes this to "spectral-norm constraints plus orthogonalized momentum" but does not isolate which mechanism matters. To better understand Moun's behavior, we run multi-seed and multi-learning-rate sweeps to decompose and stress-test the effect. First, an ablation shows the speedup comes from orthogonalization (the Newton-Schulz iteration): orthogonalize-only matches full Muon, whereas spectral-only is no faster than AdamW and is unreliable, and this verdict holds across learning rates. Second, a mechanistic analysis finds that orthogonalizing optimizers reach generalization at roughly 3x lower spectral norm and, controlling for how much the embedding actually moves, settle into a lower-norm solution rather than simply perturbing the embedding less. Third, reducing the Newton-Schulz iteration count from five to one accelerates reaching the threshold but makes the grokked solution fragile, prone to transient collapse, with fragility that grows with learning rate; a single iteration is fast and stable only at small learning rate, while the canonical five iterations are the learning-rate-robust choice. We also show spectral scaling can be dropped at no measured cost. A methodological thread runs throughout: under a stability-aware metric, "faster" claims about grokking optimizers can invert, so we report both first-crossing and sustained-grok times. To support reproducibility, we release our full training and analysis code at https://github.com/louiswang524/muon-grokking-frontier

</details>


### [176] [Scaling Closed-Loop Feature Channel Configuration with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20516)
*Tolgay Atinc Uzun,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究将基于大语言模型的闭环通道配置搜索从稀疏采样扩展到密集采样（每周期250个候选网络，共8个周期2000个生成候选，最终获得462个有效CIFAR-100评估），验证了优化信号的可迁移性，并揭示了新的架构规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证初始稀疏采样下观察到的基于大语言模型的通道配置优化行为能否迁移到密集采样场景，并探索在更多生成网络被评估时是否会出现额外的架构规律。

Method: 将搜索规模扩展至每微调周期250个候选网络，共8个完整周期生成2000个候选，经任务和元数据过滤后获得462个已验证的CIFAR-100评估，分析准确率趋势、参数效率和架构规律。

Result: 每周期平均准确率呈正向线性趋势（斜率9.87e-4，p=0.043）；最佳准确率从0.3144提升至0.3676；最佳模型参数量仅11.8M（早期高性能模型为166.5M）；41.8%的已验证候选使用非2的幂次通道宽度；最强模型呈现结构化通道分配模式（中等早期宽度+扩展的中后期块）。

Conclusion: 初始研究观察到的通道搜索信号在密集采样下可迁移，并且扩展的搜索范围揭示了新的架构规律，证明了该方法的可扩展性和有效性。

Abstract: Promising initial results in closed-loop large-language-model-based channel-configuration search demonstrated that neural-network widths can be optimized directly through executable code generation and accuracy feedback. However, those results were obtained from a relatively sparse set of valid evaluations, leaving open whether the observed optimization behavior transfers to a denser sampling regime and whether additional architectural regularities emerge when more generated networks are evaluated. To test this, the same search setting is scaled to 250 candidate networks per fine-tuning cycle. The analysis covers 2000 generated candidates from 8 complete cycles, yielding 462 verified CIFAR-100 evaluations after task and metadata filtering. Per-cycle mean accuracy exhibits a positive linear trend with slope 9.87e-4 (p=0.043), while the high-performing frontier improves more strongly: the best observed accuracy increases from 0.3144 to 0.3676, and both the top-5 and top-10 cycle-level means exhibit positive trends. The scaled run also reveals improved parameter efficiency. The best model reaches 0.3676 with 11.8M parameters, compared with an early high-performing model at 0.3144 with 166.5M parameters. Beyond accuracy, the larger sample exposes architectural regularities that were difficult to assess from sparse observations. Non-power-of-two channel widths occur in 41.8% of verified candidates, and the strongest models share structured channel-allocation patterns characterized by moderate early widths and expanded middle or later blocks. These findings indicate that the channel-search signal observed in the initial study transfers

</details>


### [177] [Multimodal CoLRAG-TF: Triple-Filtered Retrieval for Complex PDFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20517)
*Takato Yasuno*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Multimodal CoLRAG-TF 是一个四轴融合架构，通过集成文本嵌入、BM25、知识图谱三元组过滤和图像相似度，在异构PDF（日本灾害教训文档）上实现了高召回率（0.9909）和多跳推理质量提升（71.6%），其中三元组轴占主导权重（α=0.44）以对抗词汇偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构PDF集合上的检索增强生成面临多模态内容、领域专业术语以及跨散落证据的多跳推理需求等挑战，现有方法难以有效应对。

Method: 提出四轴融合架构（文本嵌入、BM25、知识图谱三元组过滤、图像相似度），构建包含2,403个块的多模态索引，利用混合OCR流水线和LLM生成图像字幕，提取11,414个OpenIE三元组并用FAISS索引，采用HippoRAG2启发的粗到精检索器（卷→章→块），并通过贝叶斯优化融合权重（三元组轴α=0.44）。

Result: 在457对基准上达到0.9909的检索召回率，多跳答案相似度相比单跳查询提升71.6%，并展示了图像到课程管线的可行性。

Conclusion: 三元组过滤的多模态融合对于嘈杂异构PDF上的结构化推理至关重要，该框架可推广至灾害领域之外。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over heterogeneous PDF collections remains challenging due to multimodal content, domain-specific terminology, and the need for multi-hop reasoning across dispersed evidence. We present Multimodal CoLRAG-TF, a four-axis fusion architecture that integrates dense text embeddings, BM25 keyword matching, knowledge-graph triple filtering, and image-based similarity for robust retrieval over complex documents. Our system constructs a multimodal index of 2,403 blocks extracted from 43 Japanese disaster lesson PDFs, supported by a hybrid OCR pipeline and LLM-based caption generation. To enhance compositional reasoning, we extract 11,414 OpenIE triples and index them with FAISS, enabling sub-second triple lookup and hierarchical propagation of relevance signals. A HippoRAG2-inspired coarse-to-fine retriever (volume $\to$ chapter $\to$ block) narrows the search space before final fusion scoring. Bayesian optimization over fusion weights reveals that the triple axis must dominate ($α_\text{triple} = 0.44$) to counteract lexical bias and sustain multi-hop retrieval quality. Evaluated on a 457-pair benchmark, Multimodal CoLRAG-TF achieves a Retrieval Recall of 0.9909 and a 71.6$\%$ improvement in multi-hop answer similarity over single-hop queries. An image-to-lesson pipeline using a vision LLM further demonstrates the applicability of the approach to visual inputs. These results show that triple-filtered multimodal fusion is essential for structured reasoning over noisy, heterogeneous PDFs and provides a general framework applicable beyond the disaster domain.

</details>


### [178] [Adaptive Depth in Looped Transformers: Diagnosing Learned Halting Gates and Trajectory Readouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20519)
*Andrei Cristian Popescu,Haitz Sáez de Ocáriz Borde,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了循环Transformer中的自适应深度问题，发现联合训练的门控机制会干扰轨迹形成，导致自适应计算性能下降；而固定先验深度监督结合事后置信度读出，往往能取得更好或相当的效果。问题根源在于联合门控训练产生的轨迹质量差，而非门控表达能力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环Transformer中，学习到的停时目标使用单一退出分布同时作为推理停时规则和训练深度权重，导致退出选择与轨迹形成相互纠缠：门控不仅选择使用哪个循环状态，还决定每个中间状态的监督强度。这种纠缠可能使自适应计算性能下降，源于读出器、诱导轨迹或二者的交互作用。

Method: 通过轨迹-读出器视角系统研究循环Transformer中的自适应深度，在受控合成任务（模算术、二进制奇偶校验）和大规模Ouro-1.4B/2.6B检查点上进行实验。对比固定先验深度监督（无输入相关停时策略）与可学习门控，以及事后置信度读出与线性/MLP门控。

Result: 固定先验深度监督能产生对难度敏感的轨迹，中间状态暴露有用的停时信号；简单的事后置信度读出往往匹配或优于可学习的线性/MLP门控。在冻结轨迹上拟合门控可定位失败原因：主要源于联合门控训练诱导的轨迹，而非门控表达能力有限。Ouro评估中预训练门控具有竞争力但并非均匀帕累托最优，平均退出深度的减少可转化为实际推理时间节省。

Conclusion: 循环Transformer中的自适应深度应被视为轨迹形成与退出读出的联合问题，而非仅门控学习问题，这一区分在先前学习停时的工作中常被隐式忽略。

Abstract: Looped Transformers increase test-time computation by repeatedly applying a shared recurrent block. Learned halting objectives in looped Transformers typically use a single exit distribution both as the inference-time stopping rule and as the training-time weighting of per-depth losses. This entangles exit selection with trajectory formation: the gate not only chooses which recurrent state to use, but also determines how strongly each intermediate state is supervised. Consequently, poor adaptive-compute performance can arise from the readout, the induced trajectory, or their interaction. We study adaptive depth in looped Transformers through this trajectory--readout lens, across controlled synthetic tasks (modular arithmetic and binary parity) and large-scale Ouro-1.4B and 2.6B checkpoints. We find that fixed-prior depth supervision, which shapes the trajectory without an input-dependent halting policy, produces difficulty-aware trajectories whose intermediate states expose useful stopping signals, and that simple post-hoc confidence readouts often match or outperform learned linear and MLP gates. Fitting gates on frozen trajectories localizes the failure: it appears to stem mainly from the trajectory induced by joint gate training rather than from limited gate expressivity. The same pattern is present in Ouro evaluations, where pretrained ponder gates are competitive but not uniformly Pareto-optimal, and measured latency confirms that the resulting reductions in average exit depth translate into practical inference-time savings. Our systematic diagnostic evaluation reframes adaptive depth in looped Transformers as a joint problem of trajectory formation and exit readout, rather than gate learning alone, highlighting a distinction that prior learned-halting work has often left implicit.

</details>


### [179] [Generative Bayesian Filtering for State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20521)
*Lei Cao,Sihang Feng,Jixin Yan,Tao Sun,Naichen Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出生成式贝叶斯滤波（GBF），用预训练的条件变分自编码器（CVAE）替代传统线性-高斯观测模型，将滤波问题转化为基于分数的采样问题，在合成数据和实际工业/医疗应用中提升了状态估计精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统滤波方法（如卡尔曼滤波）依赖线性-高斯等简单观测模型，无法刻画高维传感器信号中日益非线性和异质的模式，亟需更灵活的滤波框架。

Method: GBF用CVAE参数化的条件生成模型替代传统观测模型；在线推理中执行贝叶斯预测-更新递归，将测量更新建模为结合动力学先验与CVAE似然的后验采样问题，进而转化为基于分数的采样问题。

Result: 在合成数据集、制造系统监控和心律失常诊断等实际应用中，GBF相比基线方法在状态估计准确性和鲁棒性上均有提升。

Conclusion: GBF通过融合生成模型与贝叶斯滤波，同时具备了生成模型的灵活性和集成的不确定性量化能力，为非线性高维动态系统滤波提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The state of a dynamic system evolves over time, switching among several latent modes that govern its observable behavior. Filtering methods infer the latent state from observations. Classical filtering approaches, including Kalman filters, typically rely on simple observation models, such as linear-Gaussian models, that are incapable of characterizing the increasingly nonlinear and heterogeneous patterns in high-dimensional sensor signals. To tackle the challenge, we propose Generative Bayesian Filtering (GBF), a filtering framework that replaces restrictive observation models with pretrained conditional generative models parametrized by conditional variational autoencoders (CVAE). For online inference, GBF performs a Bayesian prediction-update recursion in which the measurement update is formulated as a posterior sampling problem that combines the dynamical prior with the CVAE-induced likelihood. The resulting filtering problem is then transformed into a score-based sampling problem, which naturally inherits the flexibility from generative models and the uncertainty quantification capabilities from ensembling. Experiments on synthetic datasets and real-world applications involving manufacturing system monitoring and arrhythmia diagnosis demonstrate that GBF improves state estimation accuracy and robustness relative to baseline approaches.

</details>


### [180] [External Clustering Validation by the Homogeneity-Parsimony Trade-off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20799)
*Andreas Tiffeau-Mayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于信息瓶颈原理的聚类同质性（homogeneity）与简约性（parsimony）归一化评分，以量化聚类对类别标签的信息性与不必要碎片化之间的权衡。通过数学证明和实例展示该评分在聚类细化下具有单调性，并扩展到基于集合匹配和配对计数的版本，统一了常用评估标准，在配对设置下同质性-简约性权衡可恢复二元分类器的ROC曲线。实验展示了其在特征选择和算法比较中的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标量指标常掩盖聚类评估中的根本权衡：聚类应既对类标签有信息量，又避免不必要的碎片化。需要一种能够清晰量化这一权衡的归一化评分方法。

Method: 基于信息瓶颈原理进行改进（不奖励有损压缩），定义同质性和简约性的归一化评分；通过数学证明和实例验证评分在聚类细化下的单调性；将信息论框架从香农熵扩展到基于集合匹配和配对计数的版本，揭示与常用评估标准的统一关系。

Result: 该评分在聚类细化下具有直观的单调性（区别于已有方案）；配对设置下同质性-简约性权衡可恢复二元分类器的ROC曲线；在特征选择和算法比较中能清晰识别聚类操作点和帕累托最优解。

Conclusion: 提出的同质性和简约性评分框架提供了一个统一的、原理性的聚类评估方法，通过联合考虑两个得分可以更全面地理解聚类性能并做出更好决策。

Abstract: Scalar metrics are often used to evaluate clusterings against known classes, but they can obscure a fundamental trade-off: clusterings should be informative about class labels while avoiding unnecessary fragmentation. Here we describe normalized scores of cluster homogeneity and parsimony that quantify this trade-off. These scores build on the information bottleneck principle, modified to not reward lossy compression. We show by example and mathematical proof that our definitions of these scores have the intuitive property of varying monotonically under cluster refinement in contrast to related proposals. Extending the information-theoretic framework beyond Shannon entropies, we furthermore derive set-matching and pair-based counterparts of the homogeneity and parsimony scores. These unify commonly used evaluation criteria and show that, in the pair-based setting, the homogeneity-parsimony trade-off recovers the receiver operating characteristic of binary classifiers. We demonstrate the framework's utility for feature selection and algorithm comparison, illustrating how considering scores jointly can clarify clustering operating points and identify Pareto-optimal solutions.

</details>


### [181] [Do Active SAE Feature Planes Carry More Holonomy? A Preregistered Reversal in Gemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20522)
*Larry Richards*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在Gemma 2 2B模型中测试了"holonomy集中在激活稀疏自编码器(SAE)特征平面"的预测，结果被证伪：激活特征平面的holonomy反而低于混合特征控制组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证语义集中假说(semantic-concentration prediction)的具体操作性实现，即holonomy是否集中在激活的SAE特征平面上。

Method: 在Gemma 2 2B模型的最后一层（layer-12到layer-13）残差流读取点上，使用受限雅可比传输规则携带局部框架绕小环运动测量holonomy，并用面积归一化旋转量。所有设计、重要性阈值、分析和判定规则均在预注册后冻结，测量结果检查前不可更改。

Result: 预测被反向证伪：激活特征平面的holonomy低于匹配的混合特征控制组，调整后对数对比度为-0.29439（95%区间[-0.43989, -0.14889]）。幅度解释不成立，且由于共同支持失败，随机、混合特征和激活特征平面的三向排序在匹配幅度下无法定义。

Conclusion: 这是一个狭窄、可审计的操作性反转结果，而非因果性主张（即"意义抑制holonomy"）。原因尚不明确，激活强度几何、特征参与度、字典几何、匹配中心位移、激活流形邻近性和传输剪切均为活态替代解释。

Abstract: This paper tests whether holonomy concentrates on active sparse-autoencoder (SAE) feature planes in Gemma 2 2B, a concrete operationalization of the broader semantic-concentration prediction. Holonomy is measured at the final-token layer-12 to layer-13 residual-stream readout by carrying a local frame around small loops using the instrument's restricted-Jacobian transport rule, then normalizing the resulting rotation by enclosed area. The design, materiality threshold, analysis, and verdict rules were preregistered and frozen before the analysed measurements were inspected. The prediction was falsified in reverse: active-feature planes carried less holonomy than matched mixed-feature controls, with an adjusted log contrast of -0.29439 and 95% interval [-0.43989, -0.14889]. A magnitude-only explanation was not supported in this design, while the three-way ordering across random, mixed-feature, and active-feature planes was undefined at matched magnitude because common support failed. Post-freeze diagnostics at the same readout supported the area law on a small validation subset, bounded matched-center displacement under a simple paired regression, and identified transport distortion as a live mechanism or confound. The result is therefore a narrow, auditable operational reversal, not a causal claim that meaning suppresses holonomy. The cause remains open, with activation-strength geometry, degree of feature engagement, dictionary geometry, matched-center displacement, activation-manifold proximity, and transport shear as live alternatives.

</details>


### [182] [Semantic-Aware Task Clustering for Constructive and Cooperative Multi-Tasking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21426)
*Ahmad Halimi Razlighi,Maximilian H. V. Tillmann,Edgar Beck,Bho Matthiesen,Armin Dekorsy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种语义感知的任务聚类方法用于协作多任务语义通信（CMT-SemCom），通过分两阶段优化（语义聚类+组内端到端联合训练）来确保任务间的建设性协作，避免负迁移，相比无聚类多任务和单独训练基准提升了准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CMT-SemCom中，协作多任务既可能是建设性的也可能是破坏性的，取决于任务间的语义关系。为抑制破坏性协作和负迁移，需要一种语义感知的任务聚类方法，将语义对齐的任务聚类后再进行联合训练。

Method: 提出两阶段序贯优化框架：(i) 基于层次密度空间聚类（如HDBSCAN）的语义聚类问题，在短时初始训练后进行一次聚类；(ii) 聚类内部进行端到端联合CMT-SemCom学习。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提框架能有效缓解破坏性协作和负迁移，相比无聚类多任务训练和单独训练基线，获得了准确率提升。

Conclusion: 语义感知的任务聚类是确保CMT-SemCom中建设性协作的关键，通过先聚类再联合训练的方式可以显著提升任务执行性能。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-task semantic communication (CMT-SemCom) improves task execution performance by leveraging shared representations. However, as we demonstrated in [1], cooperative multi-tasking can be either constructive or destructive, depending on the semantic relationships among tasks. To ensure constructive cooperation, we propose a semantic-aware task clustering method for CMT-SemCom. We have formulated a sequential multi-stage optimization problem in which semantically aligned tasks are clustered once after a short initial training phase, and then end-to-end (E2E) joint training is conducted exclusively within the discovered groups. Specifically, the problem decomposes into two stages: (i) a semantic clustering problem leveraging hierarchical density-based spatial clustering, and (ii) an intra-cluster E2E CMT-SemCom learning problem. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively mitigates destructive cooperation and negative transfer, yielding accuracy gains compared to unclustered multi-tasking and individual training baselines.

</details>


### [183] [Uncertainty-Aware Trust Estimation for Multi-LLM Systems via Structured Expert Judgement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20529)
*Jiawei Zheng,Jiazhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将多LLM聚合问题视为不确定性感知的信任估计，采用Cooke风格的对数加权方法，通过上下文感知的校准问题评估专家可靠性。实验表明，在异质性和污染场景下，该方法相比传统聚合方法在准确率-可靠性平衡上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM集成方法假设所有模型同样可信，忽略不确定性质量的差异，导致在面对可靠性差异显著的异质性LLM时，朴素聚合易受不可靠或对抗性专家影响。

Method: 将多LLM聚合形式化为不确定性感知的信任估计问题；借鉴决策理论中的结构化专家判断，使用上下文感知的校准问题评估专家可靠性；采用Cooke风格的对数加权，惩罚过度自信的错误预测，奖励校准良好的专家。

Result: 在MMLU和MMLU-Pro上评估了同质、异质和污染专家面板。同质场景下各方法表现相似，但在异质性和污染场景下，Cooke加权在准确率-可靠性平衡上优于其他方法，对不可靠专家的引入具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 多LLM聚合不仅需要组合预测，还需要在不确定性下校准信任。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) ensembles are increasingly used to improve reliability by combining predictions from multiple LLMs. However, existing aggregation methods typically assume that all models are equally trustworthy, overlooking differences in uncertainty quality. This assumption is poorly suited to heterogeneous LLMs, whose reliability and capability vary significantly, making naive aggregation vulnerable to unreliable or adversarial experts. In this work, we formulate multi-LLM aggregation as a problem of uncertainty-aware trust estimation. We adapt structured expert judgment from decision theory, using context-aware calibration questions to estimate expert reliability based on the quality of its probabilistic predictions. Specifically, we employ Cooke-style log weighting, which penalises overconfident incorrect predictions and favours well-calibrated experts. We evaluate our approach on MMLU and MMLU-Pro across homogeneous, heterogeneous, and contaminated expert panels. Results show that while aggregation methods perform similarly in homogeneous settings, Cooke weighting becomes critical under heterogeneity and contamination. It achieves a superior accuracy-reliability balance and remains robust when unreliable experts are introduced. These findings suggest that Multi-LLM aggregation requires not just combining predictions, but calibrating trust under uncertainty.

</details>


### [184] [CLOE: Christoffel Loss Autoencoder for Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20530)
*Léa Billet,Louise Travé-Massuyès,Elodie Chanthery,Alexandre Gaffet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CLOE方法，将自编码器与Christoffel函数结合，通过新颖的损失函数引导自编码器学习更适合异常检测的表示，在高维表格数据上实现了优于现有方法的性能，同时保持了轻量级和低超参数调优的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半监督异常检测在过程监控、医疗和金融等领域至关重要，但轻量级方法处理高维数据时往往困难且需要仔细调优多个超参数。Christoffel函数方法虽然简单（最多一个超参数）且有扎实理论基础，但在高维场景下可扩展性差。

Method: CLOE结合自编码器进行降维，并在潜在空间中应用基于Christoffel函数的检测器。提出利用Christoffel函数指导自编码器学习的新损失函数，使其更好地捕捉正常数据分布的支持域。同时设计了原理性的检测阈值设置方法和高效的单超参数调优策略。

Result: 在多个高维表格异常检测基准上，CLOE相比现有方法取得了更优的性能，同时保留了Christoffel函数方法的轻量级和低调优优势。

Conclusion: CLOE通过将自编码器与Christoffel函数有机结合，有效解决了Christoffel函数方法在高维场景下的可扩展性问题，为半监督异常检测提供了一种高性能、轻量级且易于调优的解决方案。

Abstract: Semi-supervised anomaly detection plays a key role in diverse fields such as process monitoring, healthcare, and finance. However, lightweight methods often struggle with high-dimensional data and typically require careful tuning of multiple hyperparameters. Among existing approaches, Christoffel Function--based methods are attractive due to their simplicity, requiring at most a single hyperparameter. They also benefit from a well-established theoretical foundation that yields several interesting results for data science. However, their main limitation is poor scalability to high-dimensional settings. In this paper, we introduce CLOE, a new method that combines an autoencoder for dimensionality reduction with a Christoffel Function--based detector applied in the latent space. To better align representation learning with anomaly detection, we design a novel loss function that leverages the Christoffel Function to guide the autoencoder toward representations that better capture the support of the normal data distribution. We further propose a principled procedure to set the detection threshold and an efficient strategy to tune the single remaining hyperparameter. Experiments on multiple high-dimensional tabular anomaly detection benchmarks demonstrate that CLOE achieves superior performance compared to existing methods, while preserving the lightweight and low-tuning advantages of Christoffel Function--based approaches.

</details>


### [185] [Position: Stop Reactively Patching Your Model Every Time and Start Proactive Test-Driven AI Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20532)
*Nadine Chang,Maying Shen,Jialiang Wang,Rafid Mahmood,Jose M. Alvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了从反应式AI飞轮转向主动式测试驱动飞轮的必要性，通过建立"测试空间"将反馈数据映射到任务目标，数学证明主动式方法在长期扩展中迭代次数更少、效果更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应式AI飞轮作为主要维护机制时，忽略了错误在系统目标中的更广泛上下文，未能预见潜在的边缘情况，导致不必要的飞轮迭代；同时，由于开放世界用例的长尾特性，收集剩余错误在统计上越来越困难。

Method: 提出构建"测试空间"（test space）来将反馈数据技术性地映射到任务目标，从而将飞轮从反应式转变为主动式。通过数学证明论证主动式飞轮比反应式飞轮在长期扩展中具有更优的缩放特性。

Result: 数学证明显示主动式测试驱动飞轮能够以更少的迭代次数实现更好的长期扩展性能。

Conclusion: 主动式测试驱动飞轮是克服反应式飞轮局限性的必要方案，有助于构建更具泛化能力的AI系统。

Abstract: Many modern AI systems are designed to operate under diverse, open-ended, use-cases. To help generalize deployed systems, many deployed-system maintenance pipelines use a reactive AI flywheel that observes emerging feedback from user behavior (errors) and patches the model accordingly. However, when used as the primary maintenance mechanism, these flywheels often ignore the broader context of these errors within the system's objectives, failing to preempt potential future edge cases, which leads to more unnecessary flywheel iterations. Also, it is statistically increasingly difficult to collect remaining errors due to the long-tail nature of open-world use-cases. This position paper argues that a proactive test-driven flywheel is required to address reactive flywheel's limitations and to approach a generalizable system. We advocate for creating a "test space" to technically map feedback data to task objectives, evolving the flywheel from reactive to proactive. We augment our position by mathematically proving a proactive one achieves better long-term scaling with fewer iterations than the reactive flywheel.

</details>


### [186] [Grounding Investor Views: Neural Predicates in the Black-Litterman Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20533)
*Marcos Florencio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用神经谓词作为结构化概率机制，在Black-Litterman模型中自动生成投资观点及其不确定性估计，取代传统的主观观点设定过程，实现端到端可微分的投资组合构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Black-Litterman模型要求投资者手动指定资产收益观点及不确定性估计，该过程主观性强且难以规模化。

Method: 通过组合层次的神经谓词处理结构化金融分析数据，输出市场立场的概率分布，并将其映射为Black-Litterman模型的P矩阵、q向量和Ω矩阵；观点置信度从谓词输出分布中导出。

Result: 方法具有可解释性（投资组合权重可沿谓词逻辑链回溯至原始数据）和完全可微分性（支持端到端学习）。

Conclusion: 为Black-Litterman模型提供了一种数据驱动、可扩展且可解释的观点生成替代方案。

Abstract: Portfolio construction under the Black-Litterman model requires investors to specify views on asset returns alongside explicit uncertainty estimates -- a process that remains largely subjective and difficult to scale. We propose a formal approach in which neural predicates serve as a structured, probabilistic mechanism for view generation. In our formulation, structured financial analysis data is processed through a compositional hierarchy of neural predicates whose outputs -- probability distributions over market stances -- are mapped to the pick matrix $\mathbf{P}$, the view return vector $\mathbf{q}$, and the view uncertainty matrix $\boldsymbolΩ$ of the Black-Litterman model. View confidence is derived from predicate output distributions, providing a data-driven alternative to subjective uncertainty elicitation. The resulting approach is interpretable, in the sense that any portfolio weight can be traced back through the predicate's logical chain to the underlying data, and fully differentiable, enabling end-to-end learning.

</details>


### [187] [A Graph Neural Network approach to zero-shot Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20535)
*Alicia Tierz,Icíar Alfaro,David González,Elías Cueto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种零样本数字孪生框架，通过热力学启发的图神经网络与实时视觉感知耦合，实现几何无关的物理仿真。在无需重新训练的情况下，可跨不同物理领域（粘弹性梁大变形和粘性流体非线性晃动）进行实时精确仿真，并支持增强现实投影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预测性数字孪生在底层物理域或边界条件变化时需要大量重新训练或微调，缺乏几何泛化能力。本文旨在构建一种无需针对特定几何重新训练即可适应新几何结构的零样本数字孪生框架。

Method: (1) 基于热力学信息图神经网络（TINN）作为核心求解器，采用度量-热力学形式约束局部能量守恒和熵产非负性；(2) 辅助GNN从稀疏初始视觉边界推断不可观测场（应力、速度、能量分布），消除数值启动瞬态；(3) 连续闭环数据同化机制：深度分割网络+稀疏光流实时跟踪宏观变形和自由流体边界，动态校正自回归仿真，消除数值漂移；(4) 通过增强现实直接投影潜在力学变量。

Result: 在两个截然不同的物理场景（粘弹性梁大变形、粘性流体非线性晃动）上均展现出极端泛化能力，可在未见过的几何结构上实现精确物理仿真，无需案例特定重新训练，每帧延迟约25毫秒，满足实时性要求。

Conclusion: 本文证明了结合热力学约束图神经网络与实时视觉感知可实现零样本、几何无关的数字孪生，大幅降低了传统方法在几何变化时的计算成本，为实时物理仿真与增强现实交互提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Traditional Predictive Digital Twins often remain geometrically rigid, requiring extensive retraining or fine-tuning whenever the underlying physical domain or boundary conditions change. To overcome this limitation, we present a novel framework for \textit{Zero-Shot Digital Twins} that seamlessly couples real-time visual perception with a geometry-agnostic, physics-informed reasoning engine. At the core of our architecture is the Thermodynamics-Informed Graph Neural Network architecture, a Geometric Deep Learning solver grounded in a metriplectic thermodynamic formalism that enforces energy conservation and non-negative entropy production locally through graph message passing. The framework integrates an auxiliary Graph Neural Network to infer unobservable fields (such as stress tensors or velocity and energy distributions) directly from sparse initial visual boundaries, mitigating numerical start-up transients. To bridge the sim-to-real gap, we implement a continuous closed-loop data assimilation mechanism; the pipeline tracks macroscopic deformations and free-surface fluid boundaries in real-time using deep segmentation networks combined with sparse optical flow, dynamically correcting the autoregressive simulation rollout and eliminating numerical drift. To test the validity of our approach, we demonstrate the extreme generalization capabilities of our approach across two disparate physical regimes: the large deformations of a viscoelastic beam and the non-linear sloshing of a viscous fluid. In both scenarios, the unified framework instantiates physically accurate simulations on novel, unseen geometries without case-specific retraining, operating well within real-time latency budgets (approximately 25 ms per frame) and enabling the direct projection of latent mechanical variables via Augmented Reality.

</details>


### [188] [ReliableTableQA:How Much Supervision Does Reliability Annotation Need?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20537)
*Huei-Chung Hu,Hsin-Tai Wu,Koyo Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReliableTableQA是一个用于训练LLM标注表格QA结果统计可靠性的框架，提出了10类可靠性分类体系(R1-R10)和基于程序的数据生成管线。研究发现，仅需200个分层SFT样本即可将可靠性F1提升至0.98、UCAR降至零，且GRPO仅在SFT训练不足时才有帮助，充分SFT后GRPO无额外收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实企业分析场景中，SQL查询虽在语法上正确，但可能基于过小样本、置信区间过宽或混杂因素过多而导致结果在统计上不可靠。现有系统对此类情况均自信地回答，造成Unreliable Confident Answer Rate (UCAR)问题。亟需一个能标注表格QA结果统计可靠性的框架。

Method: (1) 提出10类可靠性分类体系(R1-R10)，涵盖小样本聚合、多重比较膨胀、分布尾部不匹配等风险；(2) 构建基于上下文无关文法的程序优先数据管线，在公开零售模式上生成50,000个可靠性标注训练样本，并划分分层SFT/GRPO数据集；(3) 进行受控实验，比较不同监督程度下可靠性标注的效果。

Result: 200个分层SFT样本即可将可靠性标志F1从0.61提升至0.98，解析率从0.52提升至1.00，UCAR降至零，在未见过的H&M零售域上Rel-F1达0.997。GRPO仅在SFT不足(100样本)时带来+0.06-0.16的精确标志集匹配提升，充分SFT后无额外收益。

Conclusion: 可靠性标注本质上是一个数据效率问题。小规模、精心分层的SFT数据已足够有效，GRPO在SFT充分时无显著贡献。该研究精确界定了强化微调在何时有效、何时无效。

Abstract: We introduce ReliableTableQA, a framework for training an LLM to annotate the statistical reliability of tabular QA results, not whether the query is answerable, but whether the computed answer is statistically meaningful. In real enterprise analytics, a syntactically correct SQL query can return a value that is based on too small a sample, has an excessively wide confidence interval, or is too confounded to support action. Existing systems answer confidently in all such cases, a failure we quantify as the Unreliable Confident Answer Rate (UCAR). We contribute (1) a ten-category reliability taxonomy (R1-R10) covering hazards such as small-sample aggregates, multiple-comparison inflation, and distribution-tail mismatch; (2) a program-first data pipeline that generates 50,000 reliability-labeled training examples from a context-free grammar over public retail schemas, with schema-stratified SFT/GRPO splits; and (3) a controlled study of how much supervision calibrated reliability annotation actually requires. We find that a small, schema-stratified SFT set is remarkably sufficient: 200 examples raise reliability-flag F1 from 0.61 to 0.98 and parse rate from 0.52 to 1.00, drive UCAR to zero, and yield a model that generalizes to an unseen retail domain (Rel-F1 0.997 on held-out H&M). Against this strong SFT baseline, GRPO, commonly assumed to be essential, helps only when SFT is under-trained (+0.06-0.16 exact-flag-set match at 100 examples, in- and out-of-distribution) and provides no measurable benefit once SFT is adequate, a null result we confirm across a hard compound-flag slice, a strict exact-match metric, and out-of-distribution evaluation. Our findings reframe reliability annotation as a data-efficiency problem and delineate precisely when reinforcement fine-tuning does and does not pay off.

</details>


### [189] [Codec-Gauge: Learning Compression-Friendly Gauges for Transformer KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20538)
*Yitao Jiang,Yaoqing Yang,Luyang Zhao,Muhao Chen,Devin Balkcom*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Codec-Gauge方法，通过后训练学习小型正交通道变换来优化KV缓存压缩，在不改变模型权重、注意力语义或后端编码规则的情况下，平均降低44%的KL散度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文Transformer推理日益依赖KV缓存压缩或量化，但现有方法未充分优化键/值向量的通道基（channel basis）以适配固定压缩后端，导致模型行为保真度受限。

Method: 提出Codec-Gauge，一种后训练缓存坐标层，学习小型正交通道变换；目标函数结合令牌-通道DCT频谱质心损失与平滑速率代理，将KV能量集中在低频面向编解码器的布局中；在现有压缩和量化后端之上运行。

Result: 在6个模型、3/4/6 bits/value设置下，zfp KL散度平均降低44.0%，优于随机、Hadamard、DCT、PCA/KLT等对照方法；同样提升block-uniform和KIVI风格量化的质量保持；在27B模型和长上下文任务中验证趋势一致。

Conclusion: 缓存坐标几何是一个实用的后训练可调变量，可在不改变模型权重、注意力语义或后端编码规则的前提下提升压缩保真度。

Abstract: Long-context Transformer inference increasingly relies on KV-cache compression or quantization. Prior rotation and transform-coding results suggest that the channel basis of each key/value vector affects how faithfully a fixed backend preserves model behavior. We introduce Codec-Gauge, a post-training cache-coordinate layer that learns small orthogonal channel transforms around existing compression and quantization backends. Its frequency-distribution objective combines a token-channel DCT spectral-centroid loss with a smooth rate proxy to concentrate KV energy in low-frequency codec-facing layouts. We evaluate actual compression and decompression using measured bytes and rolling compressed-history scoring. Across six models at $3$, $4$, and $6$ bits/value, learned gauges reduce zfp KL divergence by $44.0\%$ on average relative to raw coordinates and outperform random, Hadamard, DCT, and PCA/KLT controls. The same gauges improve quality preservation for block-uniform and KIVI-style quantization. Experiments on a 27B model and long-context task prompts reproduce the quality trend, while serial storage and timing measurements validate the implemented compressed-cache paths. These results establish cache-coordinate geometry as a practical post-training variable for improving compression fidelity without changing model weights, attention semantics, or backend coding rules.

</details>


### [190] [Leveraging Biokinetic Knowledge Priors for Data-Scarce Bioprocess Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20539)
*Kyunghoon Hur,Eunjung Jeon,Hyun Woo Kim,Gyubok Lee,Seongjun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了如何将生物动力学ODE知识注入神经网络，以解决生物制造领域因实验成本高、数据稀缺导致深度学习应用受限的问题。通过对比数据层面预训练（在模拟ODE曲线上预训练通用解码器）和架构层面注入（将ODE嵌入解码器内部）两种方法，发现在11个数据集、7种微生物物种上两者均显著优于无先验基线，且具有可替代性。模拟预训练提供了一种简单、数据高效的深度学习方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物制造领域的深度学习应用受限于数据稀缺（ bioreactor实验成本高、周期长且数据极少公开），但该领域拥有丰富的先验知识（生物动力学ODE模型已有数十年历史）。然而如何系统地将ODE知识注入神经网络此前未被充分研究。

Method: 首次系统研究了两种注入ODE知识的方法：1) 数据层面先验：在模拟ODE曲线上预训练通用解码器；2) 架构层面先验：将ODE嵌入解码器内部。在11个数据集、7种微生物物种上进行对比实验。

Result: 两种注入方法均一致优于无先验基线；核心发现是两者具有可替代性——基于模拟预训练的通用解码器性能与基于真实数据训练的全生物结构化解码器相当。

Conclusion: 模拟预训练为生物过程数据稀缺条件下的深度学习提供了一种简单、数据高效的解决方案。

Abstract: While deep learning has accelerated drug discovery, its impact on biomanufacturing has been considerably more limited. The reason is data scarcity. Bioreactor experiments are high-cost, take days to weeks, and are rarely shared in public form, leaving each research work with only a handful of experiments. The domain itself, however, is rich in prior knowledge. Biokinetic ordinary differential equation (ODE) models have described microbial growth for decades, yet how to inject this knowledge into a neural network has not been studied systematically. We present the first systematic study of how to inject this ODE knowledge into a neural network, comparing a data-level prior that pre-trains a generic decoder on simulated ODE curves against an architecture-level prior that embeds the ODE inside the decoder. Both consistently outperform no-prior baselines across 11 datasets and 7 microbial species. Our central finding is that the two are substitutable. A generic decoder pre-trained on simulation matches a fully bio-structured decoder trained on real data. Simulation pre-training therefore offers a simple, data-efficient recipe for deep learning under bioprocess data scarcity.

</details>


### [191] [From Atoms to Entropy: Optimal Noise Allocation for Diffusion Training in the Convex Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20540)
*Luca Ambrogioni,Giulio Franzese,Alberto Foresti,Gabriel Raya,Bac Nguyen,Georgios Batzolis,Yuhta Takida,Naoki Murata,Chieh-Hsin Lai,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了扩散模型训练中噪声调度（noise schedule）的统计优化框架。主要结果为：在全耦合机制下，优化的训练调度具有原子测度形式，集中在有限个噪声水平上；在解耦学习机制下，最优调度密度与生成熵率的平方根成正比。实验验证了理论预测，并展示了平方根熵调度在离散域和连续图像任务中能提升训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前扩散模型的噪声调度主要基于启发式或经验调参，缺乏理论基础。作者希望建立严格的统计框架来回答"扩散模型应该在哪些噪声水平上训练，以及分配多少权重"这一核心问题。

Method: 1) 对全耦合机制，在凸性或Polyak-Lojasiewicz假设下证明优化调度存在原子最小化器；2) 对独立学习机制，在特征-噪声解耦条件下通过随机矩阵分析推导出解耦采样密度正比于生成熵率的平方根；3) 在Dirac混合、低维流形、MNIST等可控设置中直接优化耦合目标验证理论；4) 在大规模实验中评估平方根熵调度。

Result: 优化调度的噪声水平集中在有限个点上（有限支撑）；平方根熵代理调度在神经网络模型中紧密追踪原子最优解，在全耦合参数化情况下才会失效；平方根熵调度在离散域显著提升训练效率，在连续图像上与EDM风格启发式相当。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的噪声调度可归结为统计优化问题，理论分析表明最优调度具有有限支撑结构，而平方根熵调度是一种有效的、可扩展的理论驱动替代方案。

Abstract: How should a diffusion model decide which noise levels to train on, and how much? Despite the importance of this choice, current noise schedules are based largely on heuristics or empirical tuning. Here, we develop a general statistical framework for studying asymptotically optimal noise-level allocation in diffusion training. Our first main result concerns the fully coupled regime, where information can spread between different time points. Under convexity or Polyak-Lojasiewicz-type assumptions, we show that the optimized training schedule admits an atomic minimizer, concentrated on finitely many noise levels. Our second main result specializes this framework to an idealized independent-learner regime, intended to model temporal specialization in neural networks. Under an additional feature-noise decoupling condition, a random-matrix analysis leads to an information-theoretic proxy: the decoupled sampling density is proportional to the square root of the generative entropy rate, the rate at which conditional entropy grows along the forward process. We test these predictions in controlled settings where the coupled objective can be optimized directly, including Dirac mixtures, low-dimensional manifolds, and MNIST. In these settings, the optimized schedules are consistently finite-support, while the smooth entropic proxy closely tracks the atomic optimum in neural-network models and breaks down mainly in the fully coupled parametric case, as the theory suggests. We then evaluate the entropic schedule in larger-scale experiments, where full schedule optimization is currently intractable. The results indicate that square-root entropy scheduling can substantially improve training efficiency on discrete domains and remains competitive with standard EDM-style heuristics on continuous images.

</details>


### [192] [HypNO: A Graph-Based Neural Operator with Physics-Informed Message Passing for Hyperbolic Conservation Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20541)
*Dimitrije Ždrale,Cassie An Jeng,Katie Wang,Sonia Vanier,Alexandre Bayen,Hossein Nick Zinat Matin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HypNO是一种基于图的神经算子，用于标量双曲守恒律。它直接在有限体积单元的时空图上操作，使用基于邻接的物理信息消息传递来尊重激波附近的上风性和熵可容性。在LWR和ARZ交通流模型上验证，能准确预测不同初始条件下的解快照并捕捉激波和不连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决算子学习方法在同时存在全局输运和激波形成的双曲守恒律问题上的挑战，特别是交通流模型中的激波捕捉难题。

Method: 构建基于有限体积单元时空图的神经算子HypNO，采用基于邻接因子的物理信息消息传递机制，尊重上风性和熵可容性。

Result: 在LWR和ARZ两种交通流模型上，HypNO能够准确预测多种初始条件下的解快照，并有效捕捉激波和不连续性。

Conclusion: HypNO作为图神经算子，通过物理信息消息传递有效处理双曲守恒律中的激波问题，是算子学习方法的压力测试成功案例。

Abstract: We introduce HypNO, a graph-based neural operator for scalar hyperbolic conservation laws. HypNO operates directly on a space-time graph of finite-volume cells and uses adjacency-factored, physics-informed message passing to respect upwinding and entropy admissibility near shocks. We benchmark the architecture on the Lighthill-Whitham-Richards (LWR) and Aw-Rascle-Zhang (ARZ) traffic-flow models, a stress test for operator-learning methods because of their simultaneous global transport and shock formation. HypNO predicts solution snapshots accurately across a range of initial conditions while capturing the shocks and discontinuities of the solution.

</details>


### [193] [Improving Access to Essential Medicines via Decision-Aware Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20542)
*Angel Tsai-Hsuan Chung,Jatu Abdulai,Patrick Bayoh,Lawrence Sandi,Francis Smart,Hamsa Bastani,Osbert Bastani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向低中收入国家基本药物分配问题的决策感知机器学习框架，通过多任务学习和催化先验提升样本效率与分配公平性。在塞拉利昂全国范围的部署中，该工具使治疗地区分配产品的消费量增加19%，并最终覆盖约200万妇女和五岁以下儿童，展示了机器学习在资源有限的全球健康环境中以极低成本提升效率的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低中收入国家医疗系统面临稀缺资源（尤其是基本药物）的高效公平分配挑战，而有限的高质量数据限制了传统数据驱动方法的适用性，亟需一种兼顾效率与公平且能在数据稀缺环境下工作的决策支持方案。

Method: 提出决策感知机器学习框架，集成多任务学习以确保样本效率，引入催化先验以实现公平分配；在塞拉利昂全国范围进行交错部署作为决策支持工具，并通过计量经济学评估其效果。

Result: 计量经济学评估显示，治疗地区分配产品的消费量估计增加19%；该工具随后在全国推广，覆盖约200万妇女和五岁以下儿童。

Conclusion: 机器学习方法能够在资源受限的全球健康环境中以极低成本提升效率，本工作为LMICs的药品分配问题提供了可行的数据驱动解决方案。

Abstract: A critical challenge in healthcare systems in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs) is the efficient and equitable allocation of scarce resources, particularly essential medicines. This problem is complicated by limited high-quality data, which restricts the applicability of traditional data-driven techniques. We propose a novel decision-aware machine learning framework for essential medicines allocation, which additionally leverages multi-task learning to ensure sample efficiency and catalytic priors to ensure equitable allocation. In collaboration with the Sierra Leone national government, we performed a staggered, nationwide deployment of our system as a decision support tool. Our econometric evaluation finds an estimated 19% increase in consumption of allocated products in treated districts, demonstrating its efficacy at improving access to essential medicines. Our tool was subsequently scaled nationwide, covering an estimated 2 million women and children under five. Our work demonstrates how machine learning methods can improve efficiency at very low cost in resource-constrained global health settings.

</details>


### [194] [When RLVR Shrinks the Reasoning Boundary: Diagnosing Pass@k Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20543)
*Todd Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RLVR训练会提升单次采样准确率，但可能导致重复采样下（pass@k）的覆盖度下降，即模型在高采样预算下解决的不同问题数量反而少于基座模型。本文诊断了这一"pass@k反转"现象，将其归因于边界提示词中稀有正确轨迹在有限采样中被遗漏，并提出PBA方法通过锚定基座分布来缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到RLVR训练后模型在重复采样场景下（大k）解决的独特问题数反而减少，即pass@k反转现象，这一现象缺乏系统性的诊断和解释。

Method: 提出双模态解释框架（pass@k反转源于边界提示词中稀有正确轨迹的"证据缺失"），并设计PBA方法：对含有足够基座模型正确证据的提示词进行锐化，对风险提示词锚定到基座分布。在Omni-MATH-Test和MATH500上进行GRPO对比实验，并设计3000提示词的控制诊断研究。

Result: PBA在三个训练种子下均同时提升了Pass@1和大预算覆盖度，优于标准GRPO。诊断研究一致表明：标准GRPO丢失了基座可解的边界提示词，而PBA保留了稀有验证器正样本轨迹。

Conclusion: 推理后训练不仅要决定优化的强度，还应判断哪些提示词适合优化。该pass@k反转风险同样适用于视觉-语言智能体的重复采样场景。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) can improve one-sample accuracy while making a model worse under repeated sampling. We study this pass@k inversion: after training, the policy may solve fewer distinct problems than its base model at large $k$. The failure concentrates on boundary prompts, where the base model contains rare correct trajectories that are recoverable by sampling but too sparse to reliably appear in finite RLVR rollout groups. We argue that a two-mode account explains this as an absence-of-evidence failure: rare correct trajectories may disappear before RLVR samples and reinforces them often enough. The main contribution is this diagnostic and mechanistic framing. Per-Problem Base Anchoring (PBA) is a deliberately simple proof-of-concept: sharpen prompts with sufficient frozen-base correct evidence, and anchor risky prompts to the base distribution. Across three training seeds on Omni-MATH-Test, with MATH500 as a secondary high-coverage validation benchmark, PBA improves both \PassK{1} and high-budget coverage over matched GRPO. A 3000-prompt regime-controlled diagnostic study is consistent across seeds with the expected signature: ordinary GRPO loses base-solvable boundary prompts, while PBA preserves rare verifier-positive trajectories. We use mathematical verifiers as a controlled testbed for verifier-guided optimization; the same pass@k inversion risk applies to ECCV-relevant vision-language agents when repeated visual, spatial, or chart-reasoning attempts are checked by external tools or verifiers. Reasoning post-training should decide not only how strongly to optimize, but which prompts are safe to optimize.

</details>


### [195] [Conflict Resolution under Degraded Surveillance in Air Corridors Using Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20547)
*Esrat Farhana Dulia,Syed Arbab Mohd Shihab,Caleb Adams,Ruben Del Rosario*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了基于深度Q网络的多智能体强化学习框架，用于在含噪声、延迟、不完全或暂时不可用监视信息条件下，实现异构小型无人机和电动垂直起降飞行器在三维结构化走廊内的分散式冲突消解，并在90种交通密度和最小间隔阈值组合下进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进的空中交通运营需要在监视信息存在噪声、延迟、不完整或暂时不可用时，仍能保持飞行器之间的安全间隔，现有方法在退化的监视条件下应对异构飞行器冲突消解的能力有限。

Method: 采用深度Q网络多智能体强化学习框架，为两类飞行器分别训练独立策略；使用包含14种动作（保持航向、转弯、垂直机动、降落、速度控制）的动作空间；仿真中纳入了飞行器动力学、能耗、走廊约束、观测噪声、通信延迟、信息丢失、风扰动、执行器不确定性和模型不确定性。

Result: 在90种交通密度和最小间隔阈值组合下评估发现：间隔丢失频率和时长随交通密度和间隔要求增加而增加，但多数事件在1秒内解决；安全条件下飞行器约79%时间保持运动；冲突时转弯动作占33%、保持运动29%、速度控制25%、垂直机动13%；识别出6个帕累托最优配置。

Conclusion: 该框架支持在退化监视条件下对更安全的先进空中交通冲突消解策略进行基于仿真的评估，揭示了安全性与走廊容量之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: Safe Advanced Air Mobility operations require aircraft to maintain separation when surveillance information is noisy, delayed, incomplete, or temporarily unavailable. This study develops a Deep Q-Network-based Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning framework for decentralized conflict resolution among heterogeneous small unmanned aerial vehicles and electric vertical takeoff and landing aircraft operating within a structured three-dimensional corridor. Separate policies are trained for the two aircraft categories using local observations and a 14-action space that includes maintaining course, turning, vertical maneuvering, landing, and speed control. The simulation incorporates aircraft-specific dynamics, energy use, corridor constraints, observation noise, communication delay, information dropout, wind disturbance, actuator uncertainty, and model uncertainty. The trained policies are evaluated across 90 combinations of traffic density and minimum separation thresholds. Loss-of-separation frequency and duration generally increase with traffic density and separation requirements, although most events are resolved within 1s. Under safe conditions, agents maintain their motion approximately 79% of the time. During conflicts, turning accounts for 33% of actions, followed by maintaining motion at 29%, speed control at 25%, and vertical maneuvers at 13%. Six Pareto-optimal configurations reveal trade-offs between safety and corridor capacity. The framework supports the simulation-based evaluation of safer AAM conflict-resolution strategies under degraded surveillance conditions.

</details>


### [196] [SOAP, Muon, and Beyond: Pushing LLM Pretraining Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20548)
*Mikail Khona,Aditya Vavre,Boxiang Wang,Deyu Fu,Hao Wu,Mike Chrzanowski,Bryan Catanzaro,Dheevatsa Mudigere,Jeff Pool,Michael Lightstone,Mohammad Shoeybi,Mostofa Patwary,Nima Tajbakhsh,Tijmen Blankevoort*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模LLM预训练中高阶优化器（Muon和SOAP）的计算开销和数值稳定性问题，提出了包括每步QR正交化、改进预条件策略在内的算法改进，以及基于层级分布式优化的系统实现，实验表明在数十亿参数和万亿token规模下SOAP和Muon优于AdamW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶优化器（Muon、SOAP）虽收敛更快，但计算成本高且存在数值不稳定问题（如SOAP在大批量下出现loss spike），限制了其在大规模LLM预训练中的实际应用。

Method: 1) 算法层面：提出每步QR正交化消除SOAP在大量批下的loss spike；改进预条件策略；使用update-RMS匹配实现不同优化器间的公平学习率迁移。2) 系统层面：设计兼容Megatron-LM的层级分布式优化器，平衡内存并隐藏通信开销，同时保持优化器计算的精确性，并引入特定系统级加速优化。

Result: 在数十亿参数、万亿token规模下，SOAP和Muon一致优于AdamW；在高达1亿token的批大小下，SOAP和Muon保持稳定，而AdamW性能退化。

Conclusion: 通过算法改进和高效的分布式系统实现，高阶优化器（SOAP和Muon）可以在大规模LLM预训练中实际部署并取得优于AdamW的效果，同时开源代码库以推动社区研究。

Abstract: Higher-order optimizers such as Muon and SOAP offer faster convergence than AdamW, but their computational cost and numerical stability challenges have limited adoption at scale. In this work, we adapt and enhance preconditioned gradient methods to overcome the practical challenges of large-scale LLM pretraining. We first identify instabilities in SOAP at large batch sizes and propose algorithmic modifications including per-step QR orthogonalization and improved preconditioning strategies that eliminate loss spikes and enable stable training in these regimes. We then present a unified empirical study of SOAP, Muon, and AdamW using update-RMS matching to ensure fair learning rate transfer across optimizers. As part of this analysis, we empirically evaluate the orthogonalization quality of Muon. Our experiments on multi-billion-parameter models trained on trillions of tokens reveal that SOAP and Muon consistently outperform AdamW at the scales we tested. Notably, at batch sizes of up to 100M tokens for next-token prediction, these optimizers maintain training stability and quality while AdamW degrades. To enable efficient training at large scale, we introduce a layer-wise distributed optimizer compatible with Megatron-LM. Our implementation balances memory and hides communication while avoiding approximations to the optimizer computations, thus retaining their convergence benefits. Additionally, we identify and build specific system-level improvements to further accelerate our layer-wise implementation. To support the research community, we release a codebase that contains emerging algorithms for optimization: https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/Emerging-Optimizers

</details>


### [197] [SevDiff: Severity-Conditioned Diffusion for Long-Tail Conflict Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20549)
*Eni Solomon Laughter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SevDiff，一种基于条件去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）的轨迹生成方法，能够以目标最小碰撞时间（TTC）为条件输入，生成与之匹配的车辆交互轨迹。在高速公路交织区数据集上，SevDiff在TTC目标0.5-1.5秒范围内达到100%命中率，在2.0-2.5秒达到97-99%，且生成的轨迹具有物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ADAS评估中使用的轨迹数据集严重偏向常规驾驶，真实的车辆冲突事件极为稀少，且事件越罕见，ADAS系统未能处理时的代价越高。现有生成方法只能以场景级属性（空间目标、智能体结构或自然语言对抗目标）为条件，无法接受目标TTC值作为输入并生成可量化误差的轨迹。

Method: 提出SevDiff，一种严重性条件化的去噪扩散概率模型，以目标最小TTC（Time-to-Collision）值作为标量条件信号，生成配对的车辆交互轨迹。使用命中率（hit-rate）作为评估指标，衡量生成轨迹的实际冲突严重性与请求条件的匹配程度。模型在UTE SQM-W-1高速公路交织区数据集上训练，包含468个交互窗口、1,041辆车、822,691个观测值。

Result: SevDiff在TTC目标0.5-1.5秒范围内实现100%命中率（±0.5秒容差），2.0-2.5秒达到97-99%，5.0秒时降为39%。生成的12个运动学特征中最大超出范围率仅为4.7%，超过96.5%的样本无负速度或负间隙值。

Conclusion: SevDiff能够以可控误差生成指定冲突严重性的车辆交互轨迹。命中率随目标TTC增大的退化模式在物理上可解释——反映了条件信号相对于训练先验的强度，这使得结果不是简单的通过/失败判定，而是对生成器的精度特性进行了量化表征。

Abstract: Trajectory datasets used in ADAS evaluation are heavily biased toward routine driving; genuine vehicle-to-vehicle conflict events are rare, and the rarer the event, the higher the cost when an ADAS system fails to handle it. Existing generative approaches address this imbalance by conditioning on scene-level properties - spatial goals, agent structure, or natural-language adversarial objectives - but none can accept a target Time-to-Collision (TTC) value as input and be held to producing it within a measurable error. This paper introduces SevDiff, a severity-conditioned denoising diffusion probabilistic model (DDPM) that accepts a requested minimum TTC value as a scalar conditioning signal and generates paired vehicle interaction trajectories whose realized conflict severity matches the request, evaluated through a hit-rate metric. Trained on 468 interaction windows extracted from the UTE SQM-W-1 expressway weaving-section dataset (1,041 vehicles, 822,691 observations after smoothing), SevDiff achieves 100% hit-rate within +/-0.5 s for TTC targets of 0.5-1.5 s and 97-99% at 2.0-2.5 s, with graceful degradation to 39% at TTC = 5.0 s. Generated kinematic features are physically plausible, with a maximum out-of-range rate of 4.7% across 12 features and no negative speed or gap values in more than 96.5% of samples. The hit-rate degradation pattern is physically interpretable as the strength of the conditioning signal relative to the training prior, making it a precision characterization of the generator rather than a pass/fail result.

</details>


### [198] [Beyond SBDD: Geometric Deep Learning in Polypharmacology and Multi-target Drug Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20550)
*Tianming Han,Zhijie Pan,Wenchi Ge,Qi Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了几何深度学习（GDL）在解决多靶点药物设计（多药理学）中几何约束冲突的进展，涵盖从不变图神经网络到SE(3)-等变扩散模型，系统分析了共享结合口袋表征、多靶点活性预测和从头双靶点配体生成三大核心方向，强调扩散模型与强化学习融合的结构条件生成算法，并指出多模态组学集成和几何基准设施验证的重要性，标志着药物发现从偶然探索向理性结构驱动的多药理学分子工程转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统“一药一靶”的SBDD范式因代偿信号通路和耐药性难以治疗多因素疾病，而多药理学虽提供协同策略，但理性设计同时满足多靶点几何约束的配体存在重大计算瓶颈。本文旨在将几何深度学习定位为克服这些局限性的强大集成方法。

Method: 系统调研了从不变图神经网络到SE(3)-等变扩散模型的GDL架构，通过非欧几里得分子数据捕捉3D结构相互依赖性；重点分析了共享结合口袋的几何嵌入表征、基于异构图融合的多靶点生物活性预测、以及从头双靶点配体生成；特别关注扩散模型与强化学习融合的结构条件生成算法。

Result: GDL在三个核心维度取得进展：利用几何嵌入表征共享结合口袋、通过异构图融合实现多靶点活性预测、以及从头生成双靶点配体。结构条件生成算法能自主解决竞争性结合位点间的几何冲突，多模态组学集成和几何基准设施为模型验证提供支撑。
