We name this discipline Agentic Context Management (ACM) and decompose it into five primitives: architecting, ingesting, scoping, anticipating, and compacting & consolidation. We then make the economic case: naive context accumulation grows token cost quadratically in conversation length, crude summarization buys linear cost at the price of an accuracy cliff, and only validated compaction achieves linear cost with preserved fidelity. We describe a reference implementation, Maximem Synap, that realizes the five primitives as a multi-tenant service and reports 92% on LongMemEval and 93.2% on LoCoMo under the configuration detailed in Section 6. We close with dimensions existing benchmarks do not yet capture, latency, token efficiency, and context-rot resistance, and the frontier of decision-level and organization-level context the category points toward.

</details>


### [411] [Same Dangerous Objective, Opposite Advice: Direct Exposure versus Multi-Agent Mediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21518)
*Linjun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 当前高能力LLM在直接面对危险目标时比在目标经过其他智能体转换和传递后显得更安全。研究发现，经身份和审查转换后的目标传递给用户端模型后，反而产生了与目标对齐的建议，暴露了组合安全漏洞：高能力模型可被用作自动化多阶段工作流中的用户端组件，服务于明确带有操纵性的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在直接面对危险目标与被其他智能体间接传递后的安全行为差异，揭示组合式安全漏洞。

Method: 使用OpenAI gpt-5.6-sol模型，测试25个预设镜像权衡配置文件。比较直接暴露于授权隐瞒、捏造和施压的目标，与经过Id和Censor转换后的目标（变为情感约束和重写后的目标导向意图）下，用户端Superego模型的输出行为差异。

Result: 直接暴露于操纵性目标时模型产生与目标相反的建议；经过转换后的目标传递给用户端模型后，模型产生与目标一致的建议。这种行为的反向转变与模型识别或不信任操纵性动机一致。

Conclusion: 揭示了组合安全漏洞：当前高能力模型可被用作自动化多阶段工作流中的用户端组件，服务于明确操纵性的目标，且可使原始指令、操纵授权条款和来源信息不被下游模型和终端用户察觉。

Abstract: Even a current high-capability LLM can appear safer when shown a dangerous objective directly than when other agents transform and relay its direction. Using OpenAI's gpt-5.6-sol model alias, we test 25 pre-specified mirrored trade-off profiles. Direct exposure to an objective authorizing concealment, fabrication, and pressure produced advice net opposed to its target. After an Id and Censor transformed the same objective into affect and a constraint-rewritten, target-bearing intention, the user-facing Superego---which saw the preferred direction but not the raw objective, its manipulative clauses, or its source---produced advice net aligned with the target.
  This behavioral reverse shift is consistent with the model recognizing or distrusting the manipulative motive, although we do not identify its internal mechanism. The second result exposes a compositional safety gap: a current high-capability model can be used as the user-facing component of an automated, multi-stage workflow serving an explicitly manipulative objective. The workflow can keep the raw instruction, its manipulation-authorizing clauses, and its provenance outside the downstream model's context while preserving the objective's target direction. A user with endpoint-only access likewise cannot directly inspect those upstream messages including the objective.

</details>


### [412] [MIRROR: Learning from the Other View for Multi-Modal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21552)
*Wen Ye,Yuxiao Qu,Aviral Kumar,Xuezhe Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现VLMs在不同模态（文本、图像、图文结合）下对几何问题的推理表现不一致，为此构建了ODA-Data多模态几何数据集，并提出MIRROR强化学习方法，通过让表现最好的模态作为教师指导其他模态训练，从而提升多模态推理的准确性和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs具备强推理能力，但VLMs在视觉推理（尤其是几何问题）上表现不佳，且不同模态视图（文本、图像、图文结合）会引发不一致的推理行为，标准多模态后训练未能充分利用这种互补性。

Method: 构建ODA-Data高质量配对多模态几何数据集（含文本主导、图像主导、图文结合三种视图）；提出MIRROR（模态感知的互惠推理优化）方法，通过自监督强化学习，对每个问题在所有视图下评估，选择最佳视图作为教师，利用反向KL散度目标训练其他视图。

Result: 在几何推理基准上，MIRROR相比标准强化学习取得更优的性能，且不同模态间的推理行为更加准确和一致。

Conclusion: 利用模态间互补的推理路径和失败模式，通过自监督的跨模态知识蒸馏可以有效提升VLMs的多模态推理能力。

Abstract: Unlike large language models (LLMs) that exhibit strong reasoning capabilities, vision-language models (VLMs) struggle with visual reasoning, even on geometry problems that admit equivalent text, diagram, and combined diagram+text views. We show that these views often elicit different behaviors: a model may solve a problem from text but fail on the corresponding diagram, or succeed visually while failing textually. This inconsistency suggests that different views expose complementary reasoning paths and failure modes that standard multimodal post-training does not fully exploit. To study and exploit this phenomenon, we construct ODA-Data, a high-quality paired multimodal geometry dataset with text-dominant, image-dominant, and combined image+text views of the same problems, together with splits for training and evaluating modality-dependent reasoning behaviors. We then develop Modality-Informed Reciprocal Reasoning Optimization (MIRROR), a reinforcement learning approach for improving multimodal reasoning via self supervision. For each problem, MIRROR evaluates the model under all views, selects the best-performing view as a teacher, and trains other views with a reverse-KL objective towards the teacher. Across reasoning benchmarks that evaluate on geometry problems, MIRROR improves over standard RL and yields more accurate and consistent behavior across modalities

</details>


### [413] [Beyond Sycophancy: Structured Resistance and Compliance in LLM Moral Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21558)
*Baihui Wang,Bernard Koch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨大型语言模型在受到社会影响时如何更新判断，发现其遵循与人类社会心理学类似的三维结构：观点距离、来源归因和联盟结构。研究将谄媚（sycophancy）重新定位为更广泛的判断更新过程的一种表现，并提出区分建设性信念修正与谄媚性服从的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将谄媚视为一维失败模式，但模型需要在吸收他人观点与坚持合理道德判断之间取得平衡，需要更深入理解社会校准（social calibration）背后的认知机制。

Method: 通过三项实验研究，系统操纵观点距离（模型初始立场与输入观点之间的差异）、来源归因（观点是否被标注为模型自身的先前判断）和联盟结构（群体压力形态），观察模型判断修正的模式。

Result: 模型的判断修正沿三个维度结构化：1）更易接受邻近立场；2）更受标注为自身先前判断的观点影响；3）对群体压力的响应具有差异性。这三个维度与人类社会心理学中的经典现象平行。

Conclusion: 谄媚只是社会影响下更广泛的判断更新过程的一种表达形式；该框架为区分建设性信念修正与谄媚性服从提供了原则性基础，有助于在道德相关的交互中实现更好的对齐。

Abstract: Building socially calibrated large language models, which can learn from others without simply yielding to them, requires more than reducing sycophancy as a one-dimensional failure mode. Models must distinguish when to incorporate others' perspectives from when to maintain a well-grounded moral judgment. We study the broader resistance-compliance process governing this distinction. Across three studies, we show that models' judgment revision is structured along three dimensions that parallel classic phenomena in human social psychology: the distance between an incoming view and the model's initial position, the source attribution of that view, and the coalition structure supporting it. Models are generally more receptive to nearby positions, more influenced by views presented as their own prior judgments, and differently responsive to group pressure. These findings recast sycophancy as one expression of a broader judgment-updating process shaped by social influence. Our framework provides a principled basis for distinguishing constructive belief revision from sycophantic compliance, thereby supporting better alignment in morally consequential interactions.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [414] [A generalized shape function approach for multimaterial topology optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20784)
*Swagatam Islam Sarkar,Nikhil Singh,Anupam Saxena,Prabhat Kumar*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种广义形状函数（gSF）方法，利用紧凑设计空间实现多材料拓扑优化，可支持大量材料（2D达24种，3D达15种），并通过自然坐标的密度滤波与投影方案保证物理有效性，在刚性和柔顺机构设计中均表现出良好的收敛性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拓扑优化方法在材料数量增多时面临设计空间膨胀、物理一致性难以保证以及可扩展性受限等问题。本文旨在提出一种不受候选材料数量实际限制、可扩展至大规模工程应用的多材料拓扑优化方法。

Method: 基于一维（线性）、二维（双线性）和三维（三线性）形状函数，推广至广义n维（n-线性）形状函数以映射多材料单纯形域。将形状函数的自然坐标作为设计变量来确定材料密度，并数学证明其满足重心性质。对自然坐标直接进行密度滤波与投影，证明其物理等效性且保持重心性质。采用伴随变量法进行灵敏度分析，并利用移动渐近线法（MMA）求解优化问题。

Result: 在2D和3D刚性结构设计中分别实现了多达24种和15种材料，柔顺机构设计中同样展示了多材料配置。目标函数历史曲线显示平滑收敛。方法在多种算例中验证了其有效性、通用性和成功率。

Conclusion: 所提出的gSF方法消除了对候选材料数量的实际限制，具有良好的可扩展性，适用于大规模工程应用中的多材料拓扑优化问题。

Abstract: This paper presents a generalized shape function (gSF) approach for multi-material topology optimization that utilizes a compact design space to produce optimized configurations featuring a large number of materials. Building upon 1D (linear), 2D (bilinear), and 3D (trilinear) shape functions, generalized nD (n-linear) shape functions are conceptualized to map the multi-material simplex domain. Natural coordinates of these shape functions are considered the design variables used to determine the material densities. These densities are mathematically proven to satisfy the essential barycentric properties, guaranteeing a physically valid material interpolation space. Furthermore, we demonstrate that applying density filtering directly to the natural coordinates is mathematically equivalent to filtering the densities themselves, and that the tailored projection scheme preserves these vital barycentric properties in the projected states. The versatility, efficacy, and success of the gSF approach are demonstrated across various 2D and 3D stiff-structure (SS) and compliant-mechanism (CM) design problems. Strain energy is minimized for SS, whereas a multicriteria objective is minimized for CMs with given volume constraints. Sensitivity analysis is performed using the adjoint variable method, and the optimization problem is solved using the method of moving asymptotes. Results for SSs and CMs in 2D and 3D, respectively, up to 24 and 15 different materials, are presented. Objective history plots indicate smooth convergence. The proposed approach removes practical restrictions on the number of candidate materials, offering excellent scalability for large-scale engineering applications.

</details>


### [415] [Chemical Chain-of-Thought Functions as a Hallucination-Prone Molecular Scratchpad](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20935)
*Jiatong Li,Yuxuan Ren,Weida Wang,Xiaoyong Wei,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 化学推理语言模型在链式思维推理中普遍存在幻觉现象，正确回答常与虚构的结构性声明并存；但这些推理痕迹并非无关紧要，而是发挥着"分子草稿本"的功能，不同模型以各自形式（SMILES片段、骨架提示等）承载结构信息，其中部分结构草稿对生成结果具有因果性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究化学推理语言模型在链式思维推理中的忠实性，验证推理过程中的结构声明是否真实反映了分子的实际结构，以及这些推理痕迹是否真正对回答正确性起到因果作用。

Method: 跨四种推理模型家族（Chem-R、ether-0、ChemDFM-R等）在十二项化学任务上进行系统性分析，通过归因分析研究不同模型推理痕迹的"草稿本"功能，并对Chem-R模型的SMILES草稿进行扰动实验以检验其因果重要性。

Result: (1) 幻觉普遍存在，正确回答常伴随虚构结构声明；(2) 不同模型以特定形式（Chem-R和ether-0用SMILES片段，ChemDFM-R用骨架/位置/命名线索）承载结构信息；(3) 扰动Chem-R的SMILES草稿会降低生成质量，表明结构草稿具有因果负载作用，而口头结构声明则基本无效。

Conclusion: 化学链式思维既非忠实的解释机制，也非纯粹的事后合理化，而是一个容易产生幻觉的分子草稿本。这警示不应将CoT视为忠实推理的直接证据，并推动在答案评估之外引入过程级监督。

Abstract: Chemical reasoning language models are expected to derive molecular answers through faithful chain-of-thought (CoT). However, across four reasoning model families and twelve chemistry tasks, hallucination is widespread and largely decoupled from answer correctness: correct answers often coexist with fabricated structural claims absent from the relevant molecules. Yet this does not make the reasoning trace computationally irrelevant. Attribution analyses suggest a shared scratchpad function expressed in model-specific forms: Chem-R and ether-0 rely on fragmented SMILES drafts, whereas ChemDFM-R emphasizes scaffold, positional, and naming cues. Notably, perturbing Chem-R's SMILES sketches degrades generation, showing that structural drafts can be causally load-bearing even when verbal structural claims are largely inert. Together, these results show that chemical CoT is neither a faithful explanation nor merely a post-hoc rationalization, but a hallucination-prone molecular scratchpad. This finding cautions against treating CoT as direct evidence of faithful reasoning and motivates process-level supervision beyond answer-only evaluation.

</details>


### [416] [EuroFlood: a Python library and queryable index for the CEMS satellite-derived flood-depth archive of Europe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21126)
*Jürgen Hackl*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了EuroFlood——一个开源Python库及空间索引，用于高效查询和管理欧洲Copernicus应急管理服务（CEMS）的洪水深度栅格档案。该索引以1公里网格记录每个事件的影响范围，能够快速响应发现、重现和足迹查询，并按需检索深度栅格。通过完整性审计验证了档案覆盖率，并展示了在大陆尺度重现制图、观测与模拟洪水范围对比以及基于事件的暴露评估中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前唯一的开放大陆尺度卫星观测洪水深度档案（CEMS洪水深度图）以数千个栅格文件形式分发，每个文件的唯一空间元数据仅编码在文件名中，导致查询、检索和空间分析极为不便，缺乏标准化的空间索引支持高效发现与复用。

Method: 开发EuroFlood开源Python库，构建一个以1公里网格记录每个洪水事件覆盖情况的空间索引。该索引将事件足迹无损重建，深度栅格按需下载，大幅减少查询所需的数据量。使用独立洪水影响数据库进行完整性审计。

Result: 空间索引能以极小数据量回答发现、重现和足迹查询；无损重建归档事件足迹；完整性审计表明多数有记录的洪水事件与档案事件共存；成功展示了大陆尺度的重现期制图、观测与模拟洪水范围对比以及基于事件的暴露评估。

Conclusion: EuroFlood显著提升了CEMS洪水深度档案的可查询性和可用性，为洪水模型验证、影响评估和相关研究提供了高效工具。

Abstract: Satellite-derived observations of flood water depth support flood model validation and impact assessment, yet the only open continental-scale archive of such observations, the flood-depth maps of the Copernicus Emergency Management Service, is distributed as several thousand raster files whose sole spatial metadata is a coordinate encoded in each filename. This paper presents EuroFlood, an open-source Python library and a published spatial index that make the archive queryable. The index records which events inundated each grid cell, so discovery, recurrence, and footprint queries are answered from a small fraction of the archive volume, while depth rasters are retrieved on demand. It reproduces the archived event footprints losslessly at its grid, and a completeness audit against an independent flood-impact database shows that most documented floods co-occur with archived events. Demonstrations include continental recurrence mapping, comparison of observed with modelled flood extents, and event-based exposure assessment.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [417] [Discrete Truthful Heterogeneous Two-Facility Location: The Line and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21046)
*Panagiotis Kanellopoulos,Alexandros A. Voudouris*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文针对离散异质双设施选址问题，提出了确定性策略证明机制。在直线图上，设计了最优的4/3近似机制，填补了之前4/3到17/4的近似比间隙；在一般连通图上，设计了2近似机制，并在K_{1,3}图上证明了3/2的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双设施选址问题中，离散异质设施的策略证明机制设计存在理论空白，特别是直线图上最优近似比的上界和下界之间存在较大间隙（4/3到17/4），需要精确确定最优近似比。

Method: 提出了一种组合机制：对于n≥7的实例使用固定奇偶中位数规则保证近似比；对于小规模实例使用策略证明的局部覆盖机制进行补充处理。在一般连通图上，设计了适用于任意图的2近似策略证明机制。

Result: 直线图上的最优近似比为4/3（匹配下界）；一般连通图上的上界为2，下界为3/2（在K_{1,3}图上证明）。

Conclusion: 该工作完全解决了直线图上离散异质双设施选址的策略证明机制最优近似比问题，并为一般图提供了首个确定性策略证明机制，同时给出了下界证明。

Abstract: We study deterministic strategyproof mechanisms for discrete heterogeneous two-facility location. In our model, $n$ agents occupy distinct nodes of a connected graph and privately report non-empty approval preferences over two facilities, which must be placed at distinct nodes. The cost of an agent is her total distance from the facilities she approves, and the objective is to minimize the social cost (the total cost of the agents). For the line graph, the best possible approximation ratio of deterministic strategyproof mechanisms was previously shown to lie between $4/3$ and $17/4$. We close this gap by designing an optimal $4/3$-approximate mechanism that combines a fixed-parity median rule, which suffices for instances with $n\ge7$, and strategyproof local overrides for the remaining smaller cases. Beyond the line, we design a deterministic strategyproof $2$-approximate mechanism for every connected graph and prove a lower bound of $3/2$ on the graph $K_{1,3}$.

</details>


### [418] [Advances in STV Margin Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21178)
*Michelle Blom,Alexander Ek,Peter J. Stuckey,Vanessa Teague,Damjan Vukcevic*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进算法，用于计算单记可转移投票（STV）选举的边际（margin）下界，从而支持风险限制审计和结果可靠性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STV是一种多赢家比例代表制选举，其边际（即改变胜选结果所需的最小选票操纵数）的准确计算或合理下界对于开展风险限制审计、发现计票错误或评估选票解释误差对结果的影响至关重要，但现有算法计算效率有限。

Method: 对现有计算STV选举边际下界的算法进行了实质性改进，通过优化算法流程提升了下界的紧致性，从而在真实STV选举数据上获得更高的边际下界。

Result: 改进后的算法能够在真实STV选举中计算出更高的边际下界，使得基于不匹配（mismatch-based）的风险限制审计更加可行。

Conclusion: 本文的算法改进为STV选举提供了更紧的边际下界，推动了风险限制审计在实际STV选举中的应用，增强了选举结果的可审计性和可靠性。

Abstract: Single transferable vote (STV) is a multi-winner preferential proportional electoral system. The margin is the smallest number of ballots that need to be manipulated to alter the set of winners. If we can compute the margin of an STV election, or a reasonable lower bound on the margin, we can use recent advances in auditing research to conduct a risk-limiting audit of the election's winners. Knowledge of the margin also provides insight into whether uncovered mistakes, or a known error rate in ballot interpretation, could have influenced the outcome. This paper presents substantial improvements on an existing algorithm for computing lower bounds on the margin of an STV election. These improvements allow us to compute higher lower bounds for real STV elections, making mismatch-based risk-limiting audits more practical.

</details>
